            <div class="help-item"><i data-icon="history"></i><strong>استئناف الجلسات</strong><br>عند فتح التطبيق، ستظهر نافذة لاستئناف جلسات الاستماع والقراءة السابقة.</div>

            <div class="help-item"><i data-icon="download"></i><strong>تنزيل السور</strong><br>اضغط على زر التنزيل بجانب السورة لتنزيلها للاستماع بدون إنترنت. يمكنك اختيار "تنزيل متعدد" لتنزيل عدة سور دفعة واحدة.</div>
            <div class="help-item"><i data-icon="search"></i><strong>البحث</strong><br>استخدم مربع البحث للعثور على قارئ أو سورة أو آية بسرعة.</div>

    <script>
(function() {
    window.fontAwesomeLoaded = false;
    let isRendering = false;
    let isInitialRender = true;
    let quranTexts = null;

    var modalStack = [];
    var windowStack = [];
    var windowRelations = {
        'deleteVerifyModal': {
            parent: 'deleteSelectionModal',
            onClose: function() { showDeleteSelectionModal(); }
        },
        'deleteSelectionModal': {
            parent: 'deleteConfirmModal',
            onClose: function() { showDeleteConfirm(confirmIdx); }
        },
        'deleteConfirmModal': {
            parent: null,
            onClose: null
        },
        'surahSelectionModal': {
            parent: 'downloadConfirmModal',
            onClose: function() { showDownloadConfirm(confirmIdx); }
        },
        'downloadConfirmModal': {
            parent: null,
            onClose: null
        },
        'verifyModal': {
            parent: 'resetModal',
            onClose: function() { showResetModal(); }
        },
        'resetModal': {
            parent: null,
            onClose: null
        },
        'readerView': {
            parent: null,
            onClose: null
        },
        'tafsirModal': {
            parent: null,
            onClose: null
        },
        'sideMenu': {
            parent: null,
            onClose: null
        }
    };

    function pushWindow(id) {
        windowStack = windowStack.filter(w => w !== id);
        windowStack.push(id);
    }

    function popWindow() {
        return windowStack.pop();
    }

    function getTopWindow() {
        return windowStack.length > 0 ? windowStack[windowStack.length - 1] : null;
    }

    function getParentWindow(id) {
        return windowRelations[id]?.parent || null;
    }

    function openModalUniversal(id) {
        var modal = document.getElementById(id);
        if (!modal) return;
        if (id === 'statsTooltip' || id === 'sideMenu') {
            if (id === 'sideMenu') {
                toggleSideMenu(true);
                return;
            }
            if (id === 'statsTooltip') {
                modal.classList.add('show');
                return;
            }
            return;
        }
        pushWindow(id);
        modal.style.display = 'flex';
        modal.classList.add('show');
        lockBodyScroll();
        document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.add('blurred');
        try {
            history.pushState({ modal: id, stack: windowStack.length }, '', window.location.href);
        } catch (e) {
            window.location.hash = 'modal-' + id;
        }
    }

    function closeModalUniversal(id) {
        var modal = document.getElementById(id);
        if (!modal) return;
        if (id === 'statsTooltip') {
            modal.classList.remove('show');
            return;
        }
        if (id === 'sideMenu') {
            toggleSideMenu(false);
            return;
        }
        windowStack = windowStack.filter(w => w !== id);
        modal.style.display = 'none';
        modal.classList.remove('show');
        if (windowStack.length === 0) {
            unlockBodyScroll();
            document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.remove('blurred');
            try {
                history.replaceState({ modal: null }, '', window.location.href);
            } catch (e) {}
        } else {
            try {
                var top = windowStack[windowStack.length - 1];
                history.replaceState({ modal: top, stack: windowStack.length }, '', window.location.href);
            } catch (e) {}
        }
    }

    function closeCurrentAndReturn() {
        var topWindow = getTopWindow();
        if (!topWindow) {
            if (windowStack.length > 0) {
                windowStack = [];
                try {
                    history.replaceState({ modal: null }, '', window.location.href);
                } catch (e) {}
            }
            return false;
        }
        var modal = document.getElementById(topWindow);
        if (!modal || modal.style.display !== 'flex') {
            windowStack = windowStack.filter(function(id) {
                var m = document.getElementById(id);
                return m && m.style.display === 'flex';
            });
            return false;
        }
        var parentId = getParentWindow(topWindow);
        var parentModal = parentId ? document.getElementById(parentId) : null;
        closeModalUniversal(topWindow);
        if (parentId && windowRelations[topWindow]?.onClose) {
            if (!parentModal || parentModal.style.display !== 'flex') {
                setTimeout(function() {
                    windowRelations[topWindow].onClose();
                }, 100);
            }
            return true;
        }
        return true;
    }

    function closeModalWithoutHistory(id) {
        var modal = document.getElementById(id);
        if (!modal) return;
        windowStack = windowStack.filter(function(m) { return m !== id; });
        modal.style.display = 'none';
        modal.classList.remove('show');
        if (windowStack.length === 0) {
            unlockBodyScroll();
            document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.remove('blurred');
        }
    }

    function closeTopModal() {
        if (windowStack.length > 0) {
            return closeCurrentAndReturn();
        }
        var allWindows = [
            'deleteVerifyModal', 'deleteSelectionModal', 'deleteConfirmModal',
            'verifyModal', 'resetModal', 'downloadProgressOverlay',
            'verifyProgressModal', 'downloadConfirmModal', 'surahSelectionModal',
            'installModal', 'sideMenu', 'helpModal', 'sleepTimerModal',
            'tafsirModal', 'readerView', 'statsTooltip'
        ];
        var priority = [
            'deleteVerifyModal', 'deleteSelectionModal', 'deleteConfirmModal',
            'verifyModal', 'resetModal',
            'surahSelectionModal', 'downloadConfirmModal',
            'downloadProgressOverlay', 'verifyProgressModal',
            'tafsirModal', 'installModal', 'helpModal', 'sleepTimerModal',
            'readerView', 'sideMenu', 'statsTooltip'
        ];
        for (var i = 0; i < priority.length; i++) {
            var id = priority[i];
            var modal = document.getElementById(id);
            if (!modal) continue;
            if (id === 'readerView' && modal.classList.contains('visible')) {
                closeReader();
                return true;
            }
            if (id === 'sideMenu' && modal.classList.contains('open')) {
                toggleSideMenu(false);
                return true;
            }
            if (id === 'statsTooltip' && modal.classList.contains('show')) {
                modal.classList.remove('show');
                return true;
            }
            if (modal.style.display === 'flex') {
                closeModalUniversal(id);
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    function initHistory() {
        if (window.location.hash) {
            window.location.hash = '';
        }
        history.replaceState({ modal: null }, '', window.location.href);
    }

    initHistory();

    const colorSchemes = { gold: { gold: '#c9a84c', 'gold-light': '#e0c878', 'gold-dark': '#a8862a' }, green: { gold: '#4caf50', 'gold-light': '#81c784', 'gold-dark': '#388e3c' }, blue: { gold: '#2196f3', 'gold-light': '#64b5f6', 'gold-dark': '#1976d2' }, purple: { gold: '#9c27b0', 'gold-light': '#ba68c8', 'gold-dark': '#7b1fa2' }, red: { gold: '#f44336', 'gold-light': '#e57373', 'gold-dark': '#d32f2f' }, orange: { gold: '#ff9800', 'gold-light': '#ffb74d', 'gold-dark': '#f57c00' }, teal: { gold: '#009688', 'gold-light': '#4db6ac', 'gold-dark': '#00796b' }, pink: { gold: '#e91e63', 'gold-light': '#f06292', 'gold-dark': '#c2185b' }, indigo: { gold: '#3f51b5', 'gold-light': '#7986cb', 'gold-dark': '#303f9f' }, brown: { gold: '#795548', 'gold-light': '#a1887f', 'gold-dark': '#5d403f' } };

    function isWebView() { return window.navigator && window.navigator.userAgent && window.navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase().indexOf('wv') > -1; }
    isWebViewEnv = isWebView();

    function hexToRgb(hex) {
        const result = /^#?([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})$/i.exec(hex);
        if (result) {
            const r = parseInt(result[1], 16);
            const g = parseInt(result[2], 16);
            const b = parseInt(result[3], 16);
            return r + ', ' + g + ', ' + b;
        }
        return '201, 168, 76';
    }

    function applyColor(color) {
        currentColor = color;
        const scheme = colorSchemes[color];
        const root = document.documentElement;
        root.style.setProperty('--gold', scheme.gold);
        root.style.setProperty('--gold-light', scheme['gold-light']);
        root.style.setProperty('--gold-dark', scheme['gold-dark']);
        const rgb = hexToRgb(scheme.gold);
        root.style.setProperty('--gold-rgb', rgb);
        localStorage.setItem('accentColor', color);
        document.querySelectorAll('#colorPicker .color-dot').forEach(d => d.classList.toggle('active', d.dataset.color === color));
        const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
        if (toast) {
            toast.style.background = scheme.gold;
        }
    }

    function updateThemeColorMeta() { const meta = document.getElementById('themeColorMeta'); if (meta) meta.content = darkMode ? '#0d0d0d' : '#f5f0eb'; }

    function showToast(message, type, duration) {
        type = type || 'default';
        duration = duration || 2000;
        const t = $('toast');
        if (!t) return;
        t.className = 'toast';
        const icons = { success: '✅', error: '❌', warning: '⚠️', info: 'ℹ️', default: '' };
        const cleanMsg = message.replace(/^[✅❌⚠️ℹ️📦🗑️⏳⏹️🔄▶️⬇️🗑️]+\s*/, '');
        const icon = icons[type] || '';
        t.innerHTML = icon ? '<span>' + icon + ' ' + cleanMsg + '</span>' : '<span>' + cleanMsg + '</span>';
        t.classList.add('show');
        clearTimeout(t._timer);
        if (duration > 0) {
            t._timer = setTimeout(function() {
                t.classList.remove('show');
            }, duration);
        }
    }

    function toast(msg, duration) { showToast(msg, 'default', duration || 2000); }
    function toastSuccess(msg, duration) { showToast(msg, 'success', duration || 1800); }
    function toastError(msg, duration) { showToast(msg, 'error', duration || 3000); }
    function toastWarning(msg, duration) { showToast(msg, 'warning', duration || 2500); }
    function toastInfo(msg, duration) { showToast(msg, 'info', duration || 2000); }

    function showAlertHtml(html, onConfirm) {
        const overlay = $('customAlert'),
            msgEl = $('alertMessage');
        if (!overlay || !msgEl) return;
        msgEl.innerHTML = html;
        overlay.classList.remove('hidden');
        overlay.style.display = 'flex';
        overlay.style.background = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.7)';
        overlay.style.backdropFilter = 'blur(6px)';
        overlay.style.WebkitBackdropFilter = 'blur(6px)';
        overlay.style.zIndex = '400';
        document.body.classList.add('no-scroll');
        var cleanup = function() {
            overlay.classList.add('hidden');
            overlay.style.display = '';
            document.body.classList.remove('no-scroll');
            $('alertConfirm').removeEventListener('click', confirmHandler);
            $('alertCancel').removeEventListener('click', cancelHandler);
        };
        var confirmHandler = function() {
            cleanup();
            if (onConfirm) onConfirm();
        };
        var cancelHandler = function() {
            cleanup();
        };
        $('alertConfirm').addEventListener('click', confirmHandler);
        $('alertCancel').addEventListener('click', cancelHandler);
    }

    function getReader(id) { return readerEntries.find(function(r) { return r.id === id; }); }
    function getReaderUrl(id, idx) {
        var reader = getReader(id);
        if (!reader) return '';
        return reader.url + String(idx + 1).padStart(3, '0') + '.mp3';
    }
    function getRadioUrl(id) {
        var reader = getReader(id);
        return reader ? reader.radio : '';
    }
    function getReaderName(id) {
        var r = getReader(id);
        return r ? r.name : '';
    }
    function formatTime(s) {
        if (isNaN(s) || !isFinite(s)) return '00:00';
        var m = Math.floor(s / 60),
            sec = Math.floor(s % 60);
        return String(m).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(sec).padStart(2, '0');
    }
    function formatSize(bytes) {
        if (bytes === 0 || !bytes) return 'غير معروف';
        var sizes = ['ب', 'ك.ب', 'م.ب', 'ج.ب'];
        var i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(1024));
        return (bytes / Math.pow(1024, i)).toFixed(1) + ' ' + sizes[i];
    }

    function fetchWithTimeout(url, options) {
        options = options || {};
        var timeout = options.timeout || 5000;
        var controller = new AbortController();
        var timeoutId = setTimeout(function() {
            try { controller.abort(); } catch (e) {}
        }, timeout);
        var fetchOptions = {
            method: options.method || 'GET',
            headers: options.headers || {},
            signal: controller.signal
        };
        return fetch(url, fetchOptions).then(function(response) {
            clearTimeout(timeoutId);
            return response;
        }).catch(function(error) {
            clearTimeout(timeoutId);
            throw error;
        });
    }

    function normalizeText(text) {
        return text.replace(/[\u064B-\u065F\u0670]/g, '')
                   .replace(/آ/g, 'ا')
                   .replace(/أ/g, 'ا')
                   .replace(/إ/g, 'ا')
                   .replace(/ٱ/g, 'ا')
                   .replace(/ة/g, 'ه')
                   .replace(/ى/g, 'ي')
                   .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
                   .trim();
    }

    let networkOnline = navigator.onLine;
    let networkPollingInterval = null;
    let networkCheckAbort = null;

    function updateNetworkIndicator() {
        var indicator = document.getElementById('networkIndicator');
        var status = document.getElementById('networkStatus');
        if (!indicator || !status) return;
        if (networkOnline) {
            indicator.className = 'network-indicator online';
            status.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-wifi" style="font-size:1.2rem;"></i>';
            status.title = 'متصل بالإنترنت';
        } else {
            indicator.className = 'network-indicator offline';
            status.innerHTML = '<div style="position:relative;display:inline-block;"><i class="fas fa-wifi" style="font-size:1.2rem;opacity:0.5;"></i><i class="fas fa-times" style="position:absolute;top:-6px;right:-6px;font-size:0.8rem;color:#e74c3c;background:var(--bg);border-radius:50%;padding:1px;"></i></div>';
            status.title = 'غير متصل بالإنترنت';
        }
    }

    window.addEventListener('online', function() {
        if (!networkOnline) {
            networkOnline = true;
            updateNetworkIndicator();
            toastInfo('✅ تم استعادة الاتصال بالإنترنت');
            if (verifyCancelFlag) {
                verifyCancelFlag = false;
                startCheckingAvailability();
            }
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener('offline', function() {
        if (networkOnline) {
            networkOnline = false;
            updateNetworkIndicator();
            toastWarning('⚠️ تم فقدان الاتصال بالإنترنت');
        }
    });

    function checkNetworkViaPing() {
        if (!isWebViewEnv && networkOnline) return;
        if (networkCheckAbort) {
            try { networkCheckAbort.abort(); } catch (e) {}
            networkCheckAbort = null;
        }
        var controller = new AbortController();
        networkCheckAbort = controller;
        var timeoutId = setTimeout(function() {
            try { controller.abort(); } catch (e) {}
        }, 5000);
        var urls = [
            'https://www.google.com/favicon.ico',
            'https://www.cloudflare.com/favicon.ico',
            'https://1.1.1.1/cdn-cgi/trace'
        ];
        var success = false;
        urls.forEach(function(url, index) {
            if (success) return;
            fetch(url, {
                method: 'HEAD',
                signal: controller.signal,
                cache: 'no-store',
                mode: 'no-cors'
            }).then(function() {
                clearTimeout(timeoutId);
                if (!success) {
                    success = true;
                    if (!networkOnline) {
                        networkOnline = true;
                        updateNetworkIndicator();
                        toastInfo('✅ تم استعادة الاتصال بالإنترنت');
                        if (verifyCancelFlag) {
                            verifyCancelFlag = false;
                            startCheckingAvailability();
                        }
                    }
                    networkCheckAbort = null;
                }
            }).catch(function(error) {
                if (error.name === 'AbortError') {
                    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
                    networkCheckAbort = null;
                    return;
                }
                if (index === urls.length - 1 && !success) {
                    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
                    if (networkOnline) {
                        networkOnline = false;
                        updateNetworkIndicator();
                        toastWarning('⚠️ تم فقدان الاتصال بالإنترنت');
                    }
                    networkCheckAbort = null;
                }
            });
        });
    }

    function startNetworkPolling() {
        if (networkPollingInterval) {
            clearInterval(networkPollingInterval);
            networkPollingInterval = null;
        }
        var interval = isWebViewEnv ? 3000 : 10000;
        checkNetworkViaPing();
        networkPollingInterval = setInterval(function() {
            if (!document.hidden) {
                checkNetworkViaPing();
            }
        }, interval);
    }

    function stopNetworkPolling() {
        if (networkPollingInterval) {
            clearInterval(networkPollingInterval);
            networkPollingInterval = null;
        }
        if (networkCheckAbort) {
            try { networkCheckAbort.abort(); } catch (e) {}
            networkCheckAbort = null;
        }
    }

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function() {
        if (!document.hidden) {
            checkNetworkViaPing();
            if (!networkPollingInterval) {
                startNetworkPolling();
            }
        } else {
            if (networkPollingInterval) {
                clearInterval(networkPollingInterval);
                networkPollingInterval = null;
            }
            if (networkCheckAbort) {
                try { networkCheckAbort.abort(); } catch (e) {}
                networkCheckAbort = null;
            }
        }
    });

    document.getElementById('networkIndicator').addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (networkOnline) {
            toastInfo('متصل بالإنترنت');
        } else {
            toastWarning('غير متصل بالإنترنت');
            checkNetworkViaPing();
        }
    });

    updateNetworkIndicator();
    startNetworkPolling();

    function applyIconsToElement(el) {
        if (!el) return;
        const icon = el.dataset.icon;
        if (icon) {
            if (fontAwesomeLoaded) {
                el.className = 'fas fa-' + icon;
                el.textContent = '';
            } else {
                const fallbackMap = {
                    'play': '▶', 'pause': '⏸', 'stop': '⏹', 'download': '⬇', 'star': '★', 'book': '📖',
                    'wifi': '📶', 'search': '🔍', 'filter': '☰', 'radio': '📻', 'broadcast': '📡',
                    'hourglass-half': '⏳', 'moon': '☾', 'sun': '☀', 'adjust': '⚙', 'sliders-h': '≡',
                    'palette': '◉', 'font': 'Aa', 'arrows-alt': '↕', 'chart-simple': '▣', 'share-alt': '⇤',
                    'redo': '↻', 'list': '☰', 'check-circle': '✓', 'info-circle': 'ℹ', 'exclamation-triangle': '⚠',
                    'question-circle': '?', 'home': '⌂', 'step-backward': '◄', 'step-forward': '►',
                    'volume-up': '🔊', 'microphone': '🎤', 'book-open': '📖', 'sign-in-alt': '⌂',
                    'file-download': '⤓', 'plus-circle': '⊕', 'shield-alt': '◈', 'file-alt': '📄',
                    'redo-alt': '↻', 'arrow-right': '→', 'chevron-left': '◄', 'chevron-right': '►',
                    'minus': '−', 'plus': '+', 'sync': '⟳', 'heart': '♥', 'bookmark': '♦',
                    'headphones': '🎧', 'arrow-left': '←', 'stop-circle': '⊗', 'quran': '📖',
                    'bars': '☰', 'times': '✕', 'chevron-down': '▼', 'chevron-up': '▲', 'comment': '💬'
                };
                if (fallbackMap[icon]) {
                    el.textContent = fallbackMap[icon];
                    el.className = 'fallback-icon';
                    el.style.fontSize = 'inherit';
                    el.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
                }
            }
        }
    }

    function applyIconsToAll() {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-icon]').forEach(el => applyIconsToElement(el));
    }

    let iconLoadPromise = null;
    function loadFontAwesome() {
        if (fontAwesomeLoaded) return Promise.resolve();
        if (iconLoadPromise) return iconLoadPromise;
        iconLoadPromise = new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
            var isLoaded = localStorage.getItem('fontAwesomeLoaded') === 'true';
            var version = localStorage.getItem('fontAwesomeVersion');
            var timestamp = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('fontAwesomeTimestamp') || '0');
            var isExpired = (Date.now() - timestamp) > 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
            if (isLoaded && version === '6.5.0' && !isExpired) {
                fontAwesomeLoaded = true;
                applyIconsToAll();
                resolve();
                return;
            }
            var link = document.createElement('link');
            link.rel = 'stylesheet';
            link.href = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.0/css/all.min.css';
            link.id = 'font-awesome-css';
            document.head.appendChild(link);
            var timeoutId = setTimeout(function() {
                link.onload = null;
                link.onerror = null;
                fontAwesomeLoaded = false;
                applyIconsToAll();
                resolve();
            }, 8000);
            link.onload = function() {
                clearTimeout(timeoutId);
                fontAwesomeLoaded = true;
                try {
                    localStorage.setItem('fontAwesomeLoaded', 'true');
                    localStorage.setItem('fontAwesomeVersion', '6.5.0');
                    localStorage.setItem('fontAwesomeTimestamp', Date.now().toString());
                } catch (e) {}
                applyIconsToAll();
                resolve();
            };
            link.onerror = function() {
                clearTimeout(timeoutId);
                fontAwesomeLoaded = false;
                applyIconsToAll();
                resolve();
            };
        });
        return iconLoadPromise;
    }

    var iconObserver = null;
    function startIconObserver() {
        if (iconObserver) iconObserver.disconnect();
        iconObserver = new MutationObserver(function() {
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-icon]:not(.processed)').forEach(function(el) {
                applyIconsToElement(el);
                el.classList.add('processed');
            });
        });
        iconObserver.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        loadFontAwesome().then(function() {
            startIconObserver();
        });
    });
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() { loadFontAwesome(); });
    } else {
        loadFontAwesome();
    }

    async function syncSurahWithCache(readerId, idx) {
        const url = getReaderUrl(readerId, idx);
        if (!url) return false;
        try {
            const cache = await caches.open('quran-audio');
            const cachedResponse = await cache.match(url);
            const isInCache = !!cachedResponse;
            if (isInCache) {
                let size = 0;
                try {
                    const blob = await cachedResponse.blob();
                    size = blob.size;
                } catch (e) {}
                if (!downloaded[readerId]) downloaded[readerId] = {};
                if (!downloaded[readerId][idx] || !downloaded[readerId][idx].downloaded || downloaded[readerId][idx].size !== size) {
                    downloaded[readerId][idx] = {
                        downloaded: true,
                        size: size || 0,
                        date: new Date().toISOString()
                    };
                    debouncedSaveState();
                }
            } else {
                if (downloaded[readerId] && downloaded[readerId][idx] && downloaded[readerId][idx].downloaded) {
                    delete downloaded[readerId][idx];
                    debouncedSaveState();
                }
            }
            return isInCache;
        } catch (error) {
            return downloaded[readerId]?.[idx]?.downloaded || false;
        }
    }

    async function calculateAllSurahSizes() {
        try {
            const cache = await caches.open('quran-audio');
            const keys = await cache.keys();
            let updated = 0;
            for (const request of keys) {
                const url = request.url;
                for (const reader of readerEntries) {
                    if (url.startsWith(reader.url)) {
                        const idx = parseInt(url.replace(reader.url, '').replace('.mp3', '')) - 1;
                        if (idx >= 0 && idx < 114) {
                            const response = await cache.match(request);
                            if (response) {
                                try {
                                    const blob = await response.blob();
                                    if (blob.size > 0) {
                                        if (!downloaded[reader.id]) downloaded[reader.id] = {};
                                        if (!downloaded[reader.id][idx] || downloaded[reader.id][idx].size !== blob.size) {
                                            downloaded[reader.id][idx] = {
                                                downloaded: true,
                                                size: blob.size,
                                                date: new Date().toISOString()
                                            };
                                            updated++;
                                        }
                                    }
                                } catch (e) {}
                            }
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (updated > 0) {
                debouncedSaveState();
            }
        } catch (e) {}
    }

    async function loadQuranTexts() {
        if (quranTexts) return quranTexts;
        const cached = localStorage.getItem('quranTexts');
        if (cached) {
            try {
                quranTexts = JSON.parse(cached);
                return quranTexts;
            } catch (e) {}
        }
        quranTexts = [];
        for (let i = 1; i <= 114; i++) {
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`https://api.alquran.cloud/v1/surah/${i}/ar.alafasy`);
                const data = await response.json();
                quranTexts.push({
                    surah: i,
                    name: surahNames[i-1],
                    ayahs: data.data.ayahs.map((a, idx) => ({
                        number: idx + 1,
                        text: a.text
                    }))
                });
            } catch (e) {}
        }
        try {
            localStorage.setItem('quranTexts', JSON.stringify(quranTexts));
        } catch (e) {}
        return quranTexts;
    }

    async function searchAyahInAllQuran(query) {
        if (!quranTexts) {
            await loadQuranTexts();
        }
        if (!query || query.length < 2) return [];
        const results = [];
        const normalizedQuery = normalizeText(query);
        var searchTerms = normalizedQuery.split(' ').filter(t => t.length > 0);
        var searchByExact = searchTerms.length > 1;
        for (const surah of quranTexts) {
            for (const ayah of surah.ayahs) {
                const normalizedText = normalizeText(ayah.text);
                var match = false;
                if (searchByExact) {
                    var allTermsMatch = true;
                    for (const term of searchTerms) {
                        if (!normalizedText.includes(term)) {
                            allTermsMatch = false;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    if (allTermsMatch) {
                        match = true;
                    }
                } else {
                    if (normalizedText.includes(searchTerms[0])) {
                        match = true;
                    }
                }
                if (match) {
                    var score = 0;
                    for (const term of searchTerms) {
                        if (normalizedText.includes(term)) {
                            score++;
                        }
                    }
                    results.push({
                        surah: surah.surah,
                        surahName: surah.name,
                        ayah: ayah.number,
                        text: ayah.text,
                        score: score
                    });
                }
            }
        }
        results.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
        return results;
    }

    function highlightText(text, query) {
        const normalizedQuery = normalizeText(query);
        const terms = normalizedQuery.split(' ').filter(t => t.length > 0);
        let result = text;
        for (const term of terms) {
            const regex = new RegExp(term, 'gi');
            result = result.replace(regex, match => `<span class="highlight-term">${match}</span>`);
        }
        return result;
    }

    function renderAyahSearchResults(results, query) {
        const container = document.getElementById('readerSearchResults');
        container.innerHTML = '';
        container.classList.add('show');
        if (results.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = `
                <div class="reader-search-item" style="color:var(--text-secondary);text-align:center;padding:1rem 0;font-size:0.8rem;">
                    🔍 لا توجد آيات تطابق "<strong>${query}</strong>"
                </div>
            `;
            return;
        }
        const limited = results.slice(0, 20);
        let resultsHtml = '';
        limited.forEach((result, index) => {
            const highlightedText = highlightText(result.text, query);
            resultsHtml += `
                <div class="reader-search-item ayah-result-item" data-surah="${result.surah}" data-ayah="${result.ayah}">
                    <div class="ayah-result-content">
                        <span class="ayah-text">${highlightedText}</span>
                        <div class="ayah-meta">
                            <span class="ayah-location">📖 ${result.surahName} (الآية ${result.ayah})</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        });
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div class="ayah-results-header">
                <span class="results-title">📚 نتائج البحث: ${results.length}</span>
                <button class="close-results-btn" id="closeAyahResults">✕</button>
            </div>
            ${resultsHtml}
        `;
        container.querySelector('#closeAyahResults').addEventListener('click', function() {
            container.classList.remove('show');
            document.getElementById('searchMainInput').value = '';
            document.getElementById('searchClearBtn').classList.remove('visible');
            document.getElementById('searchResultsInfo').classList.remove('show');
        });
        container.querySelectorAll('.ayah-result-item').forEach(item => {
            item.addEventListener('click', function() {
                const surah = parseInt(this.dataset.surah);
                const ayah = parseInt(this.dataset.ayah);
                container.classList.remove('show');
                document.getElementById('searchMainInput').value = '';
                document.getElementById('searchClearBtn').classList.remove('visible');
                document.getElementById('searchResultsInfo').classList.remove('show');
                openReader(surah - 1);
                setTimeout(() => {
                    const ayahEl = document.querySelector(`.reader-ayah[data-s="${surah}"][data-a="${ayah}"]`);
                    if (ayahEl) {
                        ayahEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
                        ayahEl.style.background = 'rgba(201,168,76,0.2)';
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            ayahEl.style.background = '';
                        }, 3000);
                    }
                }, 500);
            });
        });
        applyIconsToAll();
    }

    async function updateSurahCardStatus(idx) {
        const readerId = currentReader;
        const card = document.querySelector(`.surah-card[data-idx="${idx}"]`);
        if (!card) return;
        const isInCache = await syncSurahWithCache(readerId, idx);
        const statusRow = card.querySelector('.surah-status-row');
        const badge = card.querySelector('.unavailability-badge');
        const warningBadge = card.querySelector('.warning-badge');
        const spinner = card.querySelector('.checking-spinner');
        const downloadBtn = card.querySelector('.download-btn');
        const playBtn = card.querySelector('.play-btn');
        const retryBtn = card.querySelector('.retry-check-btn');
        const status = surahAvailability[readerId]?.[idx];
        badge?.classList.remove('show');
        warningBadge?.classList.remove('show');
        spinner?.classList.remove('show');
        retryBtn?.classList.remove('show');
        downloadBtn?.classList.remove('unavailable', 'verify-failed', 'verify-pending', 'not-exist', 'downloaded');
        playBtn?.classList.remove('disabled', 'verify-pending');
        playBtn.style.opacity = '1';
        playBtn.title = 'تشغيل السورة';
        card.className = 'surah-card';
        const isDl = isInCache;
        const isPartial = downloadProgress[readerId]?.[idx] !== undefined && downloadProgress[readerId][idx] < 100;
        if (isDl) {
            downloadBtn.classList.add('downloaded');
            downloadBtn.innerHTML = '<i data-icon="check-circle"></i>';
            downloadBtn.title = 'حذف التنزيل';
            statusRow.style.display = 'none';
            playBtn.style.opacity = '1';
            playBtn.title = 'تشغيل السورة (محلية)';
            card.classList.add('status-cached');
            applyIconsToElement(downloadBtn);
            return;
        }
        if (isPartial) {
            const pct = Math.round(downloadProgress[readerId][idx]);
            downloadBtn.classList.add('downloading');
            downloadBtn.innerHTML = `
                    <svg class="progress-ring" viewBox="0 0 40 40">
                        <circle class="bg" cx="20" cy="20" r="16"/>
                        <circle class="fg" cx="20" cy="20" r="16" 
                                stroke-dasharray="100.53" 
                                stroke-dashoffset="${100.53 * (1 - pct / 100)}"/>
                    </svg>
                    <span class="progress-text">${pct}%</span>
                    <span class="cancel-icon" data-idx="${idx}">×</span>
                `;
            downloadBtn.title = 'استئناف التنزيل';
            statusRow.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }
        switch(status) {
            case 'available':
                statusRow.style.display = 'none';
                playBtn.style.opacity = '1';
                playBtn.title = 'تشغيل السورة';
                downloadBtn.innerHTML = '<i data-icon="download"></i>';
                downloadBtn.title = 'تنزيل السورة';
                card.classList.add('status-available');
                break;
            case 'cached':
                statusRow.style.display = 'none';
                playBtn.style.opacity = '1';
                playBtn.title = 'تشغيل السورة (من الذاكرة)';
                downloadBtn.innerHTML = '<i data-icon="download"></i>';
                downloadBtn.title = 'تنزيل السورة';
                card.classList.add('status-cached');
                break;
            case 'unavailable':
                badge.textContent = '❌ غير موجودة في المصدر';
                badge.classList.add('show');
                statusRow.style.display = 'flex';
                playBtn.classList.add('disabled');
                playBtn.style.opacity = '0.3';
                playBtn.title = 'السورة غير متوفرة لهذا القارئ';
                downloadBtn.classList.add('unavailable', 'not-exist');
                downloadBtn.title = 'غير متوفرة للتنزيل';
                card.classList.add('status-unavailable');
                break;
            case 'pending':
                spinner.classList.add('show');
                statusRow.style.display = 'flex';
                playBtn.classList.add('verify-pending');
                playBtn.style.opacity = '0.6';
                playBtn.title = 'جاري التحقق...';
                downloadBtn.classList.add('verify-pending');
                downloadBtn.innerHTML = '<i data-icon="download"></i>';
                downloadBtn.title = 'جاري التحقق...';
                card.classList.add('status-pending');
                break;
            case 'failed':
                warningBadge.textContent = networkOnline ? '⚠️ فشل التحقق (ضعف الاتصال)' : '⚠️ لا يوجد اتصال';
                warningBadge.classList.add('show');
                retryBtn.classList.add('show');
                retryBtn.textContent = networkOnline ? '🔄 إعادة المحاولة' : '📡 انتظر الاتصال';
                statusRow.style.display = 'flex';
                playBtn.style.opacity = '1';
                playBtn.title = 'قد تكون متوفرة، حاول التشغيل';
                downloadBtn.innerHTML = '<i data-icon="download"></i>';
                downloadBtn.title = 'قد تكون متوفرة، حاول التنزيل';
                card.classList.add('status-failed');
                break;
            default:
                statusRow.style.display = 'none';
                playBtn.style.opacity = '1';
                playBtn.title = 'تشغيل السورة';
                downloadBtn.innerHTML = '<i data-icon="download"></i>';
                downloadBtn.title = 'تنزيل السورة';
        }
        if (retryBtn && status === 'failed') {
            retryBtn.onclick = function(e) {
                e.stopPropagation();
                const idx = parseInt(this.dataset.idx);
                if (!isNaN(idx)) {
                    if (!networkOnline) {
                        toastWarning('لا يوجد اتصال بالإنترنت');
                        return;
                    }
                    if (surahAvailability[readerId]) {
                        delete surahAvailability[readerId][idx];
                    }
                    toastInfo('جاري إعادة التحقق...');
                    this.classList.add('checking');
                    checkSurahAvailability(readerId, idx);
                    let checkInterval = setInterval(() => {
                        const newStatus = surahAvailability[readerId]?.[idx];
                        if (newStatus && newStatus !== 'pending') {
                            clearInterval(checkInterval);
                            this.classList.remove('checking');
                            if (newStatus === 'available' || newStatus === 'cached') {
                                toastSuccess('✅ السورة متوفرة');
                            } else if (newStatus === 'unavailable') {
                                toastWarning('❌ السورة غير موجودة في المصدر');
                            } else if (newStatus === 'failed') {
                                toastError('⚠️ فشل التحقق، تأكد من اتصالك بالإنترنت');
                            }
                        }
                    }, 500);
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        clearInterval(checkInterval);
                        this.classList.remove('checking');
                    }, 15000);
                }
            };
        }
        applyIconsToElement(downloadBtn);
        applyIconsToElement(playBtn);
        applyIconsToElement(retryBtn);
    }

    var saveTimeout = null;
    function debouncedSaveState() {
        if (saveTimeout) clearTimeout(saveTimeout);
        saveTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
            saveState();
            saveTimeout = null;
        }, 300);
    }

    function saveState() {
        localStorage.setItem('currentReader', currentReader);
        localStorage.setItem('favSurahs', JSON.stringify(favSurahs));
        localStorage.setItem('favReaders', JSON.stringify(favReaders));
        localStorage.setItem('downloadedSurahs', JSON.stringify(downloaded));
        localStorage.setItem('playSpeed', String(speed));
        localStorage.setItem('playerVolume', String(volume));
        localStorage.setItem('quranFontSize', String(fontsize));
        localStorage.setItem('quranFont', currentFont);
        localStorage.setItem('darkMode', darkMode ? 'dark' : 'light');
        localStorage.setItem('accentColor', currentColor);
        if (currentSurah >= 0 && audio.src && audio.duration) {
            var currentTime = audio.currentTime || 0;
            if (currentTime < audio.duration) {
                localStorage.setItem('lastPlay', JSON.stringify({ 
                    reader: currentReader, 
                    surah: currentSurah, 
                    time: currentTime,
                    timestamp: Date.now()
                }));
            }
        }
    }

    function restoreLast() {
        var last = localStorage.getItem('lastPlay');
        if (last) {
            try {
                var d = JSON.parse(last);
                if (d.reader === currentReader && d.surah >= 0 && d.surah < 114) {
                    lastSurah = d.surah;
                    lastTime = d.time || 0;
                    if (lastTime < 0) lastTime = 0;
                }
            } catch (e) {}
        }
    }

    function saveReadingState(surah, ayah) {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem('lastReading', JSON.stringify({
                surah: surah,
                ayah: ayah,
                timestamp: Date.now()
            }));
        } catch (e) {}
    }

    function restoreReadingState() {
        try {
            var last = localStorage.getItem('lastReading');
            if (last) {
                var d = JSON.parse(last);
                if (d.surah >= 0 && d.surah < 114) {
                    return d;
                }
            }
        } catch (e) {}
        return null;
    }

    function toggleTheme(mode) {
        darkMode = (mode === 'dark');
        if (darkMode) document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
        else document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
        var lightBtn = document.getElementById('themeLight'),
            darkBtn = document.getElementById('themeDark');
        if (lightBtn) lightBtn.classList.toggle('active', !darkMode);
        if (darkBtn) darkBtn.classList.toggle('active', darkMode);
        updateThemeColorMeta();
        debouncedSaveState();
    }

    function loadFontFamily(font) {
        if (font === 'system') {
            currentFont = 'system';
            document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--font-family', "'Traditional Arabic','Arial',sans-serif");
            document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--font-ui', "'Tajawal','Arial',sans-serif");
            var trigger = document.getElementById('fontSelectTrigger');
            if (trigger) trigger.textContent = 'نظام (تقليدي)';
            document.querySelectorAll('#fontSelectOptions .custom-select-option').forEach(function(opt) {
                opt.classList.toggle('selected', opt.dataset.value === 'system');
            });
            localStorage.setItem('quranFont', 'system');
            return;
        }
        var linkId = 'google-font-' + font.replace(/\s/g, '');
        var link = document.getElementById(linkId);
        if (!link) {
            link = document.createElement('link');
            link.id = linkId;
            link.rel = 'stylesheet';
            link.href = 'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=' + encodeURIComponent(font) + '&display=swap';
            document.head.appendChild(link);
        }
        currentFont = font;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--font-family', "'" + font + "',serif");
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--font-ui', "'" + font + "',sans-serif");
        var trigger = document.getElementById('fontSelectTrigger'),
            options = document.querySelectorAll('#fontSelectOptions .custom-select-option');
        if (trigger) {
            var selectedOption = Array.from(options).find(function(opt) { return opt.dataset.value === font; });
            trigger.textContent = selectedOption ? selectedOption.textContent : font;
        }
        options.forEach(function(opt) { opt.classList.toggle('selected', opt.dataset.value === font); });
        localStorage.setItem('quranFont', font);
    }

    function updateFavReaderStar() {
        var star = document.getElementById('favReaderStar');
        if (star) star.classList.toggle('active', favReaders.indexOf(currentReader) !== -1);
    }

    function renderReaderList(filter) {
        var container = $('readerList');
        if (!container) return;
        var q = (filter || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        var entries = readerEntries.slice();
        if (q) entries = entries.filter(function(e) { return e.name.indexOf(q) !== -1; });
        var sorted = entries.sort(function(a, b) {
            var aFav = favReaders.indexOf(a.id) === -1 ? 1 : -1;
            var bFav = favReaders.indexOf(b.id) === -1 ? 1 : -1;
            return (aFav - bFav) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
        });
        var frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        sorted.forEach(function(entry) {
            var div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'reader-item' + (entry.id === currentReader ? ' active' : '');
            var isFav = favReaders.indexOf(entry.id) !== -1;
            var isRadioOn = isRadio && isRadioPlaying && currentReader === entry.id;
            var hasRadio = entry.radio !== null && entry.radio !== undefined && entry.radio.length > 0;
            div.innerHTML =
                '<span class="reader-name">' + entry.name + '</span>' +
                '<span class="fav-star"><i data-icon="star"></i></span>' +
                (hasRadio ?
                    '<span class="reader-radio-btn' + (isRadioOn ? ' playing' : '') + '" data-id="' + entry.id +
                    '" title="' + (isRadioOn ? 'إيقاف الراديو' : 'تشغيل الراديو') + '"><i data-icon="' + (isRadioOn ? 'stop' : 'radio') + '"></i></span>' : 
                    '<span class="reader-radio-btn disabled" title="لا يوجد راديو لهذا القارئ"><i data-icon="radio" style="opacity:0.3;"></i></span>');
            var starEl = div.querySelector('.fav-star');
            if (starEl) {
                starEl.style.opacity = isFav ? '1' : '0.3';
            }
            var radioEl = div.querySelector('.reader-radio-btn');
            if (radioEl && hasRadio) {
                radioEl.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    var readerId = this.dataset.id;
                    if (isRadio && isRadioPlaying && currentReader === readerId) {
                        stopRadio();
                    } else {
                        if (isRadio) stopRadio();
                        selectReader(readerId);
                        setTimeout(function() { startRadio(); }, 200);
                    }
                });
            }
            div.addEventListener('click', function() {
                if (!isRadio || !isRadioPlaying || entry.id !== currentReader) {
                    selectReader(entry.id);
                }
                $('readerDropdownMenu').classList.remove('show');
                $('readerDropdownBtn').classList.remove('open');
            });
            frag.appendChild(div);
        });
        container.innerHTML = '';
        container.appendChild(frag);
        applyIconsToAll();
    }

    async function selectReader(id) {
        var reader = getReader(id);
        if (!reader || id === currentReader) return;
        if (isRadio) stopRadio();
        verifyCancelFlag = false;
        currentReader = id;
        localStorage.setItem('currentReader', id);
        var nameEl = document.getElementById('selectedReaderName');
        if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = reader.name;
        updateFavReaderStar();
        renderReaderList();
        resetPlayer();
        surahAvailability[currentReader] = {};
        isInitialRender = true;
        renderAllSurahs();
        var cancelBtn = document.getElementById('cancelVerificationBtn');
        if (cancelBtn) cancelBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
        var retryBtn = document.getElementById('retryFailedBtn');
        var manualBtn = document.getElementById('manualVerifyBtn');
        if (retryBtn) retryBtn.style.display = 'none';
        if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
        setTimeout(function() {
            startCheckingAvailability();
        }, 300);
        setTimeout(function() {
            var hasResults = hasFilterResults(currentFilter);
            if (!hasResults && currentFilter !== 'all') {
                var oldFilter = currentFilter;
                var filterName = getFilterName(oldFilter);
                showAlertHtml(
                    '⚠️ القارئ "<strong>' + reader.name + '</strong>" لا يحتوي على سور تطابق الفلتر "<strong>' + 
                    filterName + '</strong>".<br><br>' +
                    'هل تريد تغيير الفلتر إلى "<strong>الكل</strong>" لعرض جميع السور؟',
                    function() {
                        currentFilter = 'all';
                        document.querySelectorAll('.filter-option').forEach(function(btn) {
                            btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.filter === 'all');
                        });
                        filterSurahs('all');
                        var noResultMsg = document.getElementById('noFilterResult');
                        if (noResultMsg) noResultMsg.remove();
                        toastInfo('✅ تم تغيير الفلتر إلى "الكل"');
                    }
                );
                var cancelBtn = document.getElementById('alertCancel');
                if (cancelBtn) {
                    cancelBtn.textContent = 'البقاء على الفلتر';
                    cancelBtn.onclick = function() {
                        document.getElementById('customAlert').classList.add('hidden');
                        document.body.classList.remove('no-scroll');
                        filterSurahs(oldFilter);
                        toastInfo('ℹ️ لا توجد سور تطابق الفلتر "' + filterName + '"');
                    };
                }
            }
        }, 500);
        toastSuccess('تم اختيار ' + reader.name);
    }

    function getFilterName(filter) {
        var names = {
            'all': 'الكل',
            'available': 'المتوفرة',
            'unavailable': 'غير المتوفرة',
            'downloaded': 'المُنزّلة',
            'notdownloaded': 'غير المُنزّلة'
        };
        return names[filter] || filter;
    }

    function hasFilterResults(filter) {
        var readerId = currentReader;
        var cards = document.querySelectorAll('.surah-card');
        var count = 0;
        cards.forEach(function(card) {
            var idx = parseInt(card.dataset.idx);
            var isDl = downloaded[readerId] && downloaded[readerId][idx] && downloaded[readerId][idx].downloaded;
            var status = surahAvailability[readerId]?.[idx];
            var show = true;
            switch(filter) {
                case 'all': show = true; break;
                case 'available': show = (status === 'available' || status === 'cached'); break;
                case 'unavailable': show = (status === 'unavailable'); break;
                case 'downloaded': show = isDl; break;
                case 'notdownloaded': show = !isDl; break;
            }
            if (show) count++;
        });
        return count > 0;
    }

    function resetFilter() {
        currentFilter = 'all';
        document.querySelectorAll('.filter-option').forEach(function(btn) {
            btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.filter === 'all');
        });
        filterSurahs('all');
        toastInfo('✅ تم عرض جميع السور');
        var noResultMsg = document.getElementById('noFilterResult');
        if (noResultMsg) noResultMsg.remove();
    }

    var speedMonitor = null,
        currentPlayTimeout = null,
        currentPlayingListener = null;

    function startRadio() {
        var url = getRadioUrl(currentReader);
        if (!url) { toastWarning('لا يوجد راديو لهذا القارئ'); return; }
        if (isRadio) { stopRadio(); return; }
        audio.pause();
        audio.src = '';
        if (speedMonitor) { clearInterval(speedMonitor);
            speedMonitor = null; }
        if (currentPlayTimeout) { clearTimeout(currentPlayTimeout);
            currentPlayTimeout = null; }
        if (currentPlayingListener) { audio.removeEventListener('playing', currentPlayingListener);
            currentPlayingListener = null; }
        isPlaying = false;
        currentSurah = -1;
        updatePlayerUI();
        radioAudio.src = url;
        radioAudio.volume = volume;
        var attempts = 0,
            maxAttempts = 3;
        var tryRadio = function() {
            var playPromise = radioAudio.play();
            if (playPromise !== undefined) {
                playPromise.then(function() {
                    isRadio = true;
                    isRadioPlaying = true;
                    toastSuccess('تم تشغيل راديو ' + getReaderName(currentReader));
                    renderReaderList();
                    updateRadioBtnUI();
                }).catch(function(error) {
                    attempts++;
                    if (attempts < maxAttempts) {
                        toastInfo('جاري إعادة محاولة تشغيل الراديو... (' + attempts + '/' + maxAttempts +
                        ')');
                        setTimeout(tryRadio, 2000);
                    } else {
                        toastError('تعذر تشغيل الراديو. تأكد من سرعة الإنترنت أو جرب لاحقاً.');
                    }
                });
            }
        };
        tryRadio();
    }

    function stopRadio() {
        radioAudio.pause();
        radioAudio.src = '';
        isRadio = false;
        isRadioPlaying = false;
        toast('تم إيقاف الراديو');
        renderReaderList();
        updateRadioBtnUI();
        var sessions = getSessions();
        var idx = sessions.findIndex(function(s) { return s.type === 'radio'; });
        if (idx !== -1) removeSession(idx);
    }

    function updateRadioBtnUI() {
        var btn = document.getElementById('radioBigBtn');
        if (!btn) return;
        if (isRadio && isRadioPlaying) {
            btn.innerHTML = '<i data-icon="stop"></i> إيقاف الراديو';
            btn.classList.add('playing');
        } else {
            btn.innerHTML = '<i data-icon="broadcast"></i> راديو';
            btn.classList.remove('playing');
        }
        applyIconsToAll();
    }

    function playSurahWithRetry(idx, attempt, startTime) {
        if (retryLock && attempt > 0) return;
        attempt = attempt || 0;
        startTime = startTime || 0;
        if (isRadio) stopRadio();
        var url = getReaderUrl(currentReader, idx);
        if (!url) { toastError('تعذر الحصول على الرابط'); return; }
        if (currentSurah === idx && audio.src && !audio.paused) {
            audio.pause();
            isPlaying = false;
            updatePlayerUI();
            return;
        }
        if (currentSurah === idx && audio.src && audio.paused) {
            audio.play();
            isPlaying = true;
            updatePlayerUI();
            return;
        }
        if (currentPlayingListener) { audio.removeEventListener('playing', currentPlayingListener);
            currentPlayingListener = null; }
        if (currentPlayTimeout) { clearTimeout(currentPlayTimeout);
            currentPlayTimeout = null; }

        var isCached = false;
        caches.open('quran-audio').then(function(cache) {
            return cache.match(url);
        }).then(function(response) {
            isCached = !!response;
            var tryPlay = function() {
                getCachedUrl(url).then(function(cachedUrl) {
                    audio.src = cachedUrl || url;
                    currentSurah = idx;
                    audio.volume = volume;
                    if (!cachedUrl) audio.load();
                    var timeoutDuration = cachedUrl ? 8000 : 10000;
                    currentPlayTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
                        if (!isPlaying && audio.paused && attempt < 2) {
                            retryLock = true;
                            toastInfo('إعادة محاولة التشغيل...');
                            if (currentPlayingListener) { audio.removeEventListener('playing',
                                    currentPlayingListener);
                                currentPlayingListener = null; }
                            currentPlayTimeout = null;
                            playSurahWithRetry(idx, attempt + 1, startTime);
                        }
                    }, timeoutDuration);

                    function onPlaying() {
                        if (currentPlayTimeout) { clearTimeout(currentPlayTimeout);
                            currentPlayTimeout = null; }
                        retryLock = false;
                        isPlaying = true;
                        updatePlayerUI();
                        if (currentPlayingListener) { audio.removeEventListener('playing',
                                currentPlayingListener);
                            currentPlayingListener = null; }
                        audio.playbackRate = speed;
                        setTimeout(function() { audio.playbackRate = speed; }, 100);
                        if (speedMonitor) clearInterval(speedMonitor);
                        speedMonitor = setInterval(function() {
                            if (audio.playbackRate !== speed && !audio.paused) audio.playbackRate =
                            speed;
                        }, 1000);
                        var infoEl = document.getElementById('playerInfo'),
                            playerEl = document.getElementById('bottomPlayer');
                        if (infoEl) infoEl.textContent = '';
                        if (playerEl) {
                            playerEl.classList.add('visible');
                            updateFooterHeight();
                        }
                        var readerName = getReaderName(currentReader);
                        var surahName = surahNames[idx];
                        if (isCached) {
                            toastSuccess(' تشغيل "' + surahName + '" من الذاكرة 📀');
                        } else {
                            toastSuccess(' تشغيل "' + surahName + '" من الإنترنت 🌐');
                        }
                        addSession('surah', currentReader, idx, startTime);
                        updatePlayerFavButton();
                        updatePlayerTitle();
                    }
                    currentPlayingListener = onPlaying;
                    audio.addEventListener('playing', onPlaying);
                    audio.currentTime = startTime;
                    audio.play().then(function() {}).catch(function(err) {
                        if (currentPlayTimeout) { clearTimeout(currentPlayTimeout);
                            currentPlayTimeout = null; }
                        if (currentPlayingListener) { audio.removeEventListener('playing',
                                currentPlayingListener);
                            currentPlayingListener = null; }
                        if (attempt < 2) {
                            retryLock = true;
                            toastInfo('إعادة محاولة التشغيل... (' + (attempt + 1) + '/2)');
                            setTimeout(function() {
                                retryLock = false;
                                playSurahWithRetry(idx, attempt + 1, startTime);
                            }, 1000 * (attempt + 1));
                        } else {
                            retryLock = false;
                            toastError('فشل التشغيل، تأكد من الاتصال بالإنترنت');
                        }
                    });
                });
            };
            tryPlay();
        }).catch(function() {
            var tryPlay = function() {
                getCachedUrl(url).then(function(cachedUrl) {
                    audio.src = cachedUrl || url;
                    currentSurah = idx;
                    audio.volume = volume;
                    if (!cachedUrl) audio.load();
                    var timeoutDuration = cachedUrl ? 8000 : 10000;
                    currentPlayTimeout = setTimeout(function() {
                        if (!isPlaying && audio.paused && attempt < 2) {
                            retryLock = true;
                            toastInfo('إعادة محاولة التشغيل...');
                            if (currentPlayingListener) { audio.removeEventListener('playing',
                                    currentPlayingListener);
                                currentPlayingListener = null; }
                            currentPlayTimeout = null;
                            playSurahWithRetry(idx, attempt + 1, startTime);
                        }
                    }, timeoutDuration);

                    function onPlaying() {
                        if (currentPlayTimeout) { clearTimeout(currentPlayTimeout);
                            currentPlayTimeout = null; }
                        retryLock = false;
                        isPlaying = true;
                        updatePlayerUI();
                        if (currentPlayingListener) { audio.removeEventListener('playing',
                                currentPlayingListener);
                            currentPlayingListener = null; }
                        audio.playbackRate = speed;
                        setTimeout(function() { audio.playbackRate = speed; }, 100);
                        if (speedMonitor) clearInterval(speedMonitor);
                        speedMonitor = setInterval(function() {
                            if (audio.playbackRate !== speed && !audio.paused) audio.playbackRate =
                            speed;
                        }, 1000);
                        var infoEl = document.getElementById('playerInfo'),
                            playerEl = document.getElementById('bottomPlayer');
                        if (infoEl) infoEl.textContent = '';
                        if (playerEl) {
                            playerEl.classList.add('visible');
                            updateFooterHeight();
                        }
                        var readerName = getReaderName(currentReader);
                        var surahName = surahNames[idx];
                        toastSuccess(' تشغيل "' + surahName + '" من الإنترنت🌐');
                        addSession('surah', currentReader, idx, startTime);
                        updatePlayerFavButton();
                        updatePlayerTitle();
                    }
                    currentPlayingListener = onPlaying;
                    audio.addEventListener('playing', onPlaying);
                    audio.currentTime = startTime;
                    audio.play().then(function() {}).catch(function(err) {
                        if (currentPlayTimeout) { clearTimeout(currentPlayTimeout);
                            currentPlayTimeout = null; }
                        if (currentPlayingListener) { audio.removeEventListener('playing',
                                currentPlayingListener);
                            currentPlayingListener = null; }
                        if (attempt < 2) {
                            retryLock = true;
                            toastInfo('إعادة محاولة التشغيل... (' + (attempt + 1) + '/2)');
                            setTimeout(function() {
                                retryLock = false;
                                playSurahWithRetry(idx, attempt + 1, startTime);
                            }, 1000 * (attempt + 1));
                        } else {
                            retryLock = false;
                            toastError('فشل التشغيل، تأكد من الاتصال بالإنترنت');
                        }
                    });
                });
            };
            tryPlay();
        });
    }

    function resetPlayer(keepExpanded) {
        audio.pause();
        audio.src = '';
        audio.playbackRate = speed;
        if (speedMonitor) { clearInterval(speedMonitor);
            speedMonitor = null; }
        if (currentPlayTimeout) { clearTimeout(currentPlayTimeout);
            currentPlayTimeout = null; }
        if (currentPlayingListener) { audio.removeEventListener('playing', currentPlayingListener);
            currentPlayingListener = null; }
        isPlaying = false;
        currentSurah = -1;
        updatePlayerUI();
        var fill = document.getElementById('playerProgressFill'),
            cur = document.getElementById('playerTimeCur'),
            dur = document.getElementById('playerTimeDur'),
            info = document.getElementById('playerInfo'),
            player = document.getElementById('bottomPlayer');
        if (fill) fill.style.width = '0%';
        if (cur) cur.textContent = '00:00';
        if (dur) dur.textContent = '00:00';
        if (info) info.textContent = '';
        if (player && !keepExpanded) {
            player.classList.remove('visible');
            player.style.bottom = '0px';
            document.body.style.paddingBottom = '5rem';
        }
        updatePlayerFavButton();
        updatePlayerTitle();
    }

    function updateFooterHeight() {
        var footer = document.getElementById('fixedFooter');
        if (!footer) return;
        var footerHeight = footer.offsetHeight;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--footer-height', footerHeight + 'px');
        if (currentSurah >= 0 && !isRadio) {
            var playerEl = document.getElementById('bottomPlayer');
            if (playerEl && playerEl.classList.contains('visible') && !playerEl.classList.contains('modal-mode')) {
                playerEl.style.bottom = footerHeight + 'px';
                document.body.style.paddingBottom = (footerHeight + playerEl.offsetHeight + 32) + 'px';
            }
        }
    }

    function updatePlayerUI() {
        var btn = $('playerPlayBtn');
        if (btn) btn.innerHTML = isPlaying ? '<i data-icon="pause"></i>' : '<i data-icon="play"></i>';
        var infoEl = $('playerInfo');
        if (infoEl) {
            if (currentSurah >= 0 && audio.src) {
                var isCached = audio.src.startsWith('blob:');
                if (isCached) {
                    infoEl.textContent = '📀 من الذاكرة';
                } else {
                    infoEl.textContent = '🌐 من الإنترنت';
                }
            } else {
                infoEl.textContent = '';
            }
        }
        var playerEl = $('bottomPlayer');
        if (playerEl) {
            if (currentSurah >= 0 && !isRadio) {
                playerEl.classList.add('visible');
                updateFooterHeight();
            } else {
                playerEl.classList.remove('visible');
                playerEl.style.bottom = '0px';
                document.body.style.paddingBottom = '5rem';
            }
        }
        updateRadioBtnUI();
        updatePlayerFavButton();
        updatePlayerTitle();
        applyIconsToAll();
    }

    function updatePlayerTitle() {
        var titleEl = document.getElementById('playerTitle'),
            subtitleEl = document.getElementById('playerSubtitle');
        if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = currentSurah >= 0 ? 'سورة ' + surahNames[currentSurah] + ' (' + (
            currentSurah + 1) + ')' : 'اختر سورة';
        if (subtitleEl) subtitleEl.textContent = currentSurah >= 0 ? getReaderName(currentReader) : '';
    }

    function updatePlayerFavButton() {
        var btn = document.getElementById('playerFavBtn');
        if (!btn) return;
        btn.classList.toggle('active', currentSurah >= 0 && favSurahs.indexOf(currentSurah) !== -1);
    }

    audio.addEventListener('timeupdate', function() {
        if (audio.duration && !isRadio) {
            var pct = (audio.currentTime / audio.duration) * 100;
            var fill = document.getElementById('playerProgressFill'),
                cur = document.getElementById('playerTimeCur'),
                dur = document.getElementById('playerTimeDur');
            if (fill) fill.style.width = pct + '%';
            if (cur) cur.textContent = formatTime(audio.currentTime);
            if (dur) dur.textContent = formatTime(audio.duration);
        }
    });

    audio.addEventListener('ended', function() {
        if (repeatMode === 'one') {
            audio.currentTime = 0;
            audio.play();
            return;
        }
        if (repeatMode === 'all') {
            var visibleSurahs = [];
            document.querySelectorAll('.surah-card').forEach(function(card) {
                if (card.style.display !== 'none') {
                    var idx = parseInt(card.dataset.idx);
                    if (!isNaN(idx)) visibleSurahs.push(idx);
                }
            });
            if (visibleSurahs.length === 0) {
                isPlaying = false;
                updatePlayerUI();
                return;
            }
            var currentIndex = visibleSurahs.indexOf(currentSurah);
            var nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % visibleSurahs.length;
            var nextSurah = visibleSurahs[nextIndex];
            var total = visibleSurahs.length;
            var position = nextIndex + 1;
            toastInfo('⏩ ' + surahNames[nextSurah] + ' (' + position + '/' + total + ')');
            playSurah(nextSurah, 0);
            return;
        }
        isPlaying = false;
        updatePlayerUI();
    });

    audio.addEventListener('play', function() { isPlaying = true;
        updatePlayerUI(); });
    audio.addEventListener('pause', function() { isPlaying = false;
        updatePlayerUI(); });

    var playBtn = $('playerPlayBtn');
    if (playBtn) playBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (!audio.src && currentSurah < 0) { toastWarning('اختر سورة أولاً'); return; }
        if (audio.paused) audio.play();
        else audio.pause();
    });

    var stopBtn = $('playerStopBtn');
    if (stopBtn) stopBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        audio.pause();
        audio.currentTime = 0;
        isPlaying = false;
        currentSurah = -1;
        audio.src = '';
        updatePlayerUI();
        var fill = document.getElementById('playerProgressFill'),
            cur = document.getElementById('playerTimeCur'),
            dur = document.getElementById('playerTimeDur'),
            info = document.getElementById('playerInfo'),
            player = document.getElementById('bottomPlayer');
        if (fill) fill.style.width = '0%';
        if (cur) cur.textContent = '00:00';
        if (dur) dur.textContent = '00:00';
        if (info) info.textContent = '';
        if (player) { player.classList.remove('visible');
            player.style.bottom = '0px';
            document.body.style.paddingBottom = '5rem'; }
        var sessions = getSessions();
        var idx = sessions.findIndex(function(s) { return s.type === 'surah'; });
        if (idx !== -1) removeSession(idx);
        toast('تم إيقاف التشغيل');
        updatePlayerFavButton();
        updatePlayerTitle();
    });

    var prevBtn = $('playerPrevBtn');
    if (prevBtn) prevBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (currentSurah > 0) {
            var visibleSurahs = [];
            document.querySelectorAll('.surah-card').forEach(function(card) {
                if (card.style.display !== 'none') {
                    var idx = parseInt(card.dataset.idx);
                    if (!isNaN(idx)) visibleSurahs.push(idx);
                }
            });
            var currentIndex = visibleSurahs.indexOf(currentSurah);
            if (currentIndex > 0) {
                playSurah(visibleSurahs[currentIndex - 1], 0);
            } else {
                toastWarning('أنت في أول سورة ضمن هذا الفلتر');
            }
        } else {
            toastWarning('لا توجد سورة حالية');
        }
    });

    var nextBtn = $('playerNextBtn');
    if (nextBtn) nextBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (currentSurah >= 0) {
            var visibleSurahs = [];
            document.querySelectorAll('.surah-card').forEach(function(card) {
                if (card.style.display !== 'none') {
                    var idx = parseInt(card.dataset.idx);
                    if (!isNaN(idx)) visibleSurahs.push(idx);
                }
            });
            var currentIndex = visibleSurahs.indexOf(currentSurah);
            if (currentIndex !== -1 && currentIndex < visibleSurahs.length - 1) {
                playSurah(visibleSurahs[currentIndex + 1], 0);
            } else {
                toastWarning('أنت في آخر سورة ضمن هذا الفلتر');
            }
        } else {
            toastWarning('لا توجد سورة حالية');
        }
    });

    var progressTrack = document.getElementById('playerProgress');
    if (progressTrack) progressTrack.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        if (!audio.duration || isRadio) return;
        var rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
        var pct = (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;
        audio.currentTime = pct * audio.duration;
    });

    var repeatBtn = $('repeatBtn');
    if (repeatBtn) repeatBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        var modes = ['off', 'one', 'all'];
        var idx = modes.indexOf(repeatMode);
        repeatMode = modes[(idx + 1) % 3];
        var labels = { off: 'إيقاف', one: 'تكرار سورة', all: 'تكرار الكل' };
        repeatBtn.innerHTML = '<i data-icon="redo"></i> ' + labels[repeatMode];
        toastInfo(labels[repeatMode]);
        applyIconsToAll();
    });

    var speedBtn = $('speedBtn');
    if (speedBtn) speedBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        var speeds = [1, 1.5, 2, 2.5, 3];
        var i = speeds.indexOf(speed);
        i = (i + 1) % speeds.length;
        speed = speeds[i];
        audio.playbackRate = speed;
        localStorage.setItem('playSpeed', String(speed));
        speedBtn.textContent = speed + 'x';
        toastInfo('السرعة: ' + speed + 'x');
    });

    var volumeSliderInline = document.getElementById('volumeSliderInline');
    if (volumeSliderInline) {
        volumeSliderInline.value = volume;
        volumeSliderInline.addEventListener('input', function(e) {
            volume = parseFloat(e.target.value);
            audio.volume = volume;
            if (radioAudio) radioAudio.volume = volume;
            localStorage.setItem('playerVolume', String(volume));
        });
    }

    var favReaderStar = document.getElementById('favReaderStar');
    if (favReaderStar) favReaderStar.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        var idx = favReaders.indexOf(currentReader);
        if (idx >= 0) {
            favReaders.splice(idx, 1);
            toastSuccess('تم إزالة القارئ من المفضلة');
        } else {
            favReaders.push(currentReader);
            toastSuccess('تمت إضافة القارئ إلى المفضلة');
        }
        updateFavReaderStar();
        renderReaderList();
        debouncedSaveState();
    });

    var playerFavBtn = document.getElementById('playerFavBtn');
    if (playerFavBtn) playerFavBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (currentSurah < 0) { toastWarning('لا توجد سورة مشغلة'); return; }
        toggleFavSurah(currentSurah);
        updatePlayerFavButton();
    });

    var radioBigBtn = document.getElementById('radioBigBtn');
    if (radioBigBtn) radioBigBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (isRadio && isRadioPlaying) { stopRadio(); } else { startRadio(); }
    });

    function openReader(idx) {
        if (idx < 0 || idx > 113) { toastError('رقم سورة غير صحيح'); return; }
        var view = $('readerView'),
            title = $('readerTitle');
        if (!view || !title) return;
        view.classList.add('visible');
        title.textContent = surahNames[idx] || 'سورة ' + (idx + 1);
        loadSurahText(idx);
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        isReaderOpen = true;
        addSession('reader', currentReader, idx, 0);
        pushWindow('readerView');
        history.pushState({ modal: 'readerView' }, '', window.location.href);
        document.getElementById('readerSearchContainer').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('readerSearchInput').value = '';
        document.getElementById('readerSearchCount').textContent = '0/0';
        clearSearchHighlights();
    }

    function closeReader() {
        var view = $('readerView');
        if (view) view.classList.remove('visible');
        document.body.style.overflow = '';
        isReaderOpen = false;
        var sessions = getSessions();
        var idx = sessions.findIndex(function(s) { return s.type === 'reader' && s.reader === currentReader; });
        if (idx !== -1) removeSession(idx);
        windowStack = windowStack.filter(function(w) { return w !== 'readerView'; });
        if (windowStack.length === 0) {
            unlockBodyScroll();
            document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.remove('blurred');
        }
        if (history.state && history.state.modal === 'readerView') {
