            history.replaceState({ modal: windowStack.length > 0 ? windowStack[windowStack.length - 1] : null }, '', window.location.href);
        }
        document.getElementById('readerSearchContainer').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('readerSearchInput').value = '';
        document.getElementById('readerSearchCount').textContent = '0/0';
        clearSearchHighlights();
    }

    var readerSearchResults = [];
    var readerSearchIndex = -1;

    document.getElementById('readerSearchToggle').addEventListener('click', function() {
        const container = document.getElementById('readerSearchContainer');
        if (container.style.display === 'none') {
            container.style.display = 'flex';
            document.getElementById('readerSearchInput').focus();
        } else {
            container.style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('readerSearchInput').value = '';
            clearSearchHighlights();
            document.getElementById('readerSearchCount').textContent = '0/0';
            readerSearchResults = [];
            readerSearchIndex = -1;
        }
    });

    function clearSearchHighlights() {
        document.querySelectorAll('.reader-ayah').forEach(el => {
            el.classList.remove('search-highlight', 'search-current');
        });
    }

    function searchInCurrentSurah(query) {
        const container = document.getElementById('readerContent');
        const ayahs = container.querySelectorAll('.reader-ayah');
        clearSearchHighlights();
        readerSearchResults = [];
        readerSearchIndex = -1;
        if (!query || query.length < 2) {
            document.getElementById('readerSearchCount').textContent = '0/0';
            return;
        }
        const normalizedQuery = normalizeText(query);
        var searchTerms = normalizedQuery.split(' ').filter(t => t.length > 0);
        var searchByExact = searchTerms.length > 1;
        ayahs.forEach((ayah, index) => {
            const text = ayah.querySelector('.ayah-text').textContent;
            const normalizedText = normalizeText(text);
            var match = false;
            if (searchByExact) {
                var allTermsMatch = true;
                for (const term of searchTerms) {
                    if (!normalizedText.includes(term)) {
                        allTermsMatch = false;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (allTermsMatch) {
                    match = true;
                }
            } else {
                if (normalizedText.includes(searchTerms[0])) {
                    match = true;
                }
            }
            if (match) {
                readerSearchResults.push(index);
                ayah.classList.add('search-highlight');
            }
        });
        if (readerSearchResults.length > 0) {
            readerSearchIndex = 0;
            highlightResult(readerSearchResults[0]);
            document.getElementById('readerSearchCount').textContent = `1/${readerSearchResults.length}`;
        } else {
            document.getElementById('readerSearchCount').textContent = `0/${readerSearchResults.length}`;
        }
    }

    function highlightResult(index) {
        const container = document.getElementById('readerContent');
        const ayahs = container.querySelectorAll('.reader-ayah');
        ayahs.forEach(el => el.classList.remove('search-current'));
        if (index >= 0 && index < ayahs.length) {
            const ayah = ayahs[index];
            ayah.classList.add('search-current');
            ayah.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
        }
    }

    document.getElementById('readerSearchInput').addEventListener('input', function() {
        const query = this.value.trim();
        searchInCurrentSurah(query);
    });

    document.getElementById('readerSearchPrev').addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (readerSearchResults.length === 0) return;
        readerSearchIndex = (readerSearchIndex - 1 + readerSearchResults.length) % readerSearchResults.length;
        highlightResult(readerSearchResults[readerSearchIndex]);
        document.getElementById('readerSearchCount').textContent = `${readerSearchIndex + 1}/${readerSearchResults.length}`;
    });

    document.getElementById('readerSearchNext').addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (readerSearchResults.length === 0) return;
        readerSearchIndex = (readerSearchIndex + 1) % readerSearchResults.length;
        highlightResult(readerSearchResults[readerSearchIndex]);
        document.getElementById('readerSearchCount').textContent = `${readerSearchIndex + 1}/${readerSearchResults.length}`;
    });

    document.getElementById('readerSearchClear').addEventListener('click', function() {
        document.getElementById('readerSearchInput').value = '';
        clearSearchHighlights();
        document.getElementById('readerSearchCount').textContent = '0/0';
        readerSearchResults = [];
        readerSearchIndex = -1;
        document.getElementById('readerSearchContainer').style.display = 'none';
    });

    document.getElementById('readerSearchInput').addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            this.value = '';
            clearSearchHighlights();
            document.getElementById('readerSearchCount').textContent = '0/0';
            readerSearchResults = [];
            readerSearchIndex = -1;
            document.getElementById('readerSearchContainer').style.display = 'none';
        }
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (readerSearchResults.length > 0) {
                readerSearchIndex = (readerSearchIndex + 1) % readerSearchResults.length;
                highlightResult(readerSearchResults[readerSearchIndex]);
                document.getElementById('readerSearchCount').textContent = `${readerSearchIndex + 1}/${readerSearchResults.length}`;
            }
        }
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        const resultsContainer = document.getElementById('readerSearchResults');
        if (resultsContainer && resultsContainer.classList.contains('show')) {
            if (!e.target.closest('#searchWrapper') && !e.target.closest('.ayah-result-item')) {
                resultsContainer.classList.remove('show');
                document.getElementById('searchMainInput').value = '';
                document.getElementById('searchClearBtn').classList.remove('visible');
                document.getElementById('searchResultsInfo').classList.remove('show');
            }
        }
    });

    var mufassirMap = { 'layer1': 'تفسير الميزان (العلامة الطباطبائي)', 'layer2': 'تفسير الميزان (العلامة الطباطبائي)',
        'layer3': 'تفسير معاصر (آية الله مكارم الشيرازي)', 'layer4': 'روايات أهل البيت (الأئمة عليهم السلام)',
        'layer5': 'تفسير مقارن (ابن كثير، الطبري، الرازي)' };
    const tafsirCache = new Map();

    async function getTafsirFromFile(surah, ayah) {
        var cacheKey = 'tafsir_' + surah;
        var data = null;
        if (tafsirCache.has(cacheKey)) {
            data = tafsirCache.get(cacheKey);
        } else {
            try {
                var raw = localStorage.getItem('quranTafsir_' + cacheKey);
                if (raw) {
                    var parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
                    if (parsed && parsed.data) {
                        data = parsed.data;
                        tafsirCache.set(cacheKey, data);
                    }
                }
            } catch (e) {}
        }
        if (!data) {
            var url = 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/MAmhaz/QuranKarim/main/Data/tafsir_' + surah + '.json';
            try {
                var response = await fetch(url);
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error('فشل التحميل');
                data = await response.json();
                tafsirCache.set(cacheKey, data);
                try {
                    localStorage.setItem('quranTafsir_' + cacheKey, JSON.stringify({ data: data, ts: Date
                            .now() }));
                } catch (e) {}
            } catch (e) {
                return { text: 'حدث خطأ في تحميل التفسير', mufassir: '' };
            }
        }
        var ayahData = data[ayah];
        if (!ayahData) return { text: 'الآية غير موجودة', mufassir: '' };
        var key = 'layer1_ar';
        var text = ayahData[key] || 'التفسير غير متوفر';
        var mufassir = mufassirMap['layer1'] || 'غير معروف';
        return { text: text, mufassir: mufassir };
    }

    function attachAyahEvents(container) {
        if (!container) return;
        container.querySelectorAll('.ayah-text').forEach(function(el) { el.style.fontSize = fontsize + 'rem'; });
        container.querySelectorAll('.ayah-text').forEach(function(el) {
            el.addEventListener('click', async function(e) {
                var parent = el.closest('.reader-ayah');
                if (!parent) return;
                var s = parent.dataset.s,
                    a = parent.dataset.a;
                currentAyahText = el.textContent;
                showTafsir(s, a);
            });
        });
    }

    function generateSurahHtml(data, idx) {
        var ayahs = data.data.ayahs;
        var basmala = (idx === 0) ? '' : (idx === 8 ? '' :
            '<div style="font-family:var(--font-family);font-size:1.3rem;text-align:center;color:var(--gold-dark);margin:.5rem 0;">بِسْمِ اللَّهِ الرَّحْمَٰنِ الرَّحِيمِ</div>'
            );
        var html = basmala;
        ayahs.forEach(function(a) {
            html += '<div class="reader-ayah" data-s="' + (idx + 1) + '" data-a="' + a.numberInSurah +
                '"><span class="ayah-num">' + a.numberInSurah +
                '</span><span class="ayah-text" data-text="' + a.text.replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + '">' + a
                .text + '</span><div class="ayah-actions"></div></div>';
        });
        return html;
    }

    async function loadSurahText(idx) {
        var container = $('readerContent');
        if (!container) return;
        var cacheKey = 'surah_' + idx;
        if (textCache.has(cacheKey)) {
            container.innerHTML = textCache.get(cacheKey);
            attachAyahEvents(container);
            applyIconsToAll();
            return;
        }
        var stored = null;
        try {
            var raw = localStorage.getItem('quranText_' + cacheKey);
            if (raw) {
                var parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
                if (parsed && parsed.html) stored = parsed.html;
            }
        } catch (e) {}
        if (stored) {
            textCache.set(cacheKey, stored);
            container.innerHTML = stored;
            attachAyahEvents(container);
            applyIconsToAll();
            return;
        }
        container.innerHTML = '<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:3rem 0;gap:1rem;"><div class="loading-spinner"></div><span style="color:var(--text-secondary);font-size:0.9rem;">جاري تحميل السورة...</span></div>';
        try {
            var res = await fetch('https://api.alquran.cloud/v1/surah/' + (idx + 1) + '/ar.alafasy');
            var data = await res.json();
            var html = generateSurahHtml(data, idx);
            container.innerHTML = html;
            textCache.set(cacheKey, container.innerHTML);
            try {
                localStorage.setItem('quranText_' + cacheKey, JSON.stringify({ html: container.innerHTML,
                    ts: Date.now() }));
            } catch (e) {}
            attachAyahEvents(container);
            applyIconsToAll();
        } catch (e) {
            container.innerHTML =
                '<div style="text-align:center;padding:2.5rem 0;color:var(--text-secondary);">فشل تحميل السورة</div>';
        }
    }

    async function showTafsir(surah, ayah) {
        var modal = $('tafsirModal'),
            title = $('tafsirTitle'),
            textEl = $('tafsirText');
        if (!modal || !title || !textEl) return;
        openModalUniversal('tafsirModal');
        var result = await getTafsirFromFile(surah, ayah);
        var surahName = surahNames[surah - 1];
        title.textContent = 'تفسير الآية ' + ayah + ' من سورة ' + surahName;
        var tafsirText = result.text;
        textEl.innerHTML = '<strong>📚 المفسر: ' + result.mufassir + '</strong>\n\n' + tafsirText;
        var ayahText = currentAyahText || 'الآية غير متوفرة';
        var shareBtn = document.getElementById('tafsirShareBtn');
        if (shareBtn) {
            shareBtn.onclick = function() {
                var shareText = '📖 الآية: ' + ayahText + '\n— سورة ' + surahName + ' (الآية ' + ayah +
                    ') —\n\n📚 المفسر: ' + result.mufassir + '\n📝 التفسير: ' + tafsirText +
                    '\n\n🕌 تطبيق القرآن الكريم\n👨‍💻 منجد أمهز\n🔗 https://mamhaz.github.io/QuranKarim\n📅 ' +
                    currentYear;
                if (navigator.share) {
                    try {
                        navigator.share({ title: 'الآية ' + ayah, text: shareText });
                    } catch (e) {}
                } else {
                    try {
                        navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareText);
                        toastSuccess('تم نسخ الآية مع التفسير');
                    } catch (e) {
                        toastError('تعذر المشاركة');
                    }
                }
            };
        }
        saveReadingState(surah, ayah);
    }

    var tafsirClose = $('tafsirClose');
    if (tafsirClose) tafsirClose.addEventListener('click', function() { closeModalUniversal('tafsirModal'); });
    var tafsirModal = $('tafsirModal');

    });

        if (e.key === ' ' && !isReaderOpen && !document.querySelector('.tafsir-modal.show')) {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (audio.paused && audio.src) audio.play();
            else if (!audio.paused && audio.src) audio.pause();
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener('popstate', function(e) {
        if (windowStack.length > 0) {
            closeCurrentAndReturn();
            e.preventDefault();
            return;
        }
        if (closeTopModal()) {
            e.preventDefault();
            return;
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener('hashchange', function(e) {
        if (closeTopModal()) {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (window.location.hash) {
                history.replaceState(null, '', window.location.pathname);
            }
        }
    });

    var fontInc = $('fontInc');
    if (fontInc) fontInc.addEventListener('click', function() {
        fontsize = Math.min(2.8, fontsize + 0.1);
        document.querySelectorAll('#readerContent .ayah-text').forEach(function(el) { el.style.fontSize =
                fontsize + 'rem'; });
        localStorage.setItem('quranFontSize', String(fontsize));
    });

    if (fontDec) fontDec.addEventListener('click', function() {
        fontsize = Math.max(0.8, fontsize - 0.1);
        document.querySelectorAll('#readerContent .ayah-text').forEach(function(el) { el.style.fontSize =
                fontsize + 'rem'; });
        localStorage.setItem('quranFontSize', String(fontsize));
    });

    var searchInput = $('searchMainInput'),
        searchClearBtn = $('searchClearBtn'),
        searchTargetReaders = $('searchTargetReaders'),
        searchTargetSurahs = $('searchTargetSurahs'),
        searchTargetAyahs = document.getElementById('searchTargetAyahs'),
        searchResultsInfo = $('searchResultsInfo'),
        readerSearchResults = document.getElementById('readerSearchResults');

    if (searchClearBtn) searchClearBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        searchInput.value = '';
        searchInput.focus();
        searchClearBtn.classList.remove('visible');
        if (searchTarget === 'readers') {
            if (readerSearchResults) readerSearchResults.classList.remove('show');
            updateSearchResultsInfo('');
        } else if (searchTarget === 'surahs') {
            renderAllSurahs();
            updateSearchResultsInfo('');
        } else if (searchTarget === 'ayahs') {
            readerSearchResults.classList.remove('show');
            updateSearchResultsInfo('');
        }
    });

    if (searchInput) searchInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
        var q = searchInput.value.trim();
        if (q.length > 0) searchClearBtn.classList.add('visible');
        else searchClearBtn.classList.remove('visible');
        if (searchTarget === 'readers') {
            updateReaderSearchResults(q);
            updateSearchResultsInfo(q);
        } else if (searchTarget === 'surahs') {
            if (readerSearchResults) readerSearchResults.classList.remove('show');
            filterSurahs(q);
            updateSearchResultsInfo(q);
        } else if (searchTarget === 'ayahs') {
            if (q.length >= 2) {
                searchAyahInAllQuran(q).then(results => {
                    renderAyahSearchResults(results, q);
                });
            } else {
                readerSearchResults.classList.remove('show');
                updateSearchResultsInfo(q);
            }
        }
    });

    function filterSurahs(q) {
        var grid = $('surahGrid');
        if (!grid) return;
        var trimmed = q.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!trimmed) {
            grid.querySelectorAll('.surah-card').forEach(function(card) { card.style.display = 'flex'; });
            var noResultMsg = document.getElementById('noSurahResult');
            if (noResultMsg) noResultMsg.remove();
            return;
        }
        var parts = trimmed.split(' ').filter(function(p) { return p.length > 0; });
        var cards = grid.querySelectorAll('.surah-card');
        var found = false;
        if (parts.length === 1) {
            var term = parts[0];
            cards.forEach(function(card) {
                var name = card.querySelector('.surah-name').textContent.toLowerCase();
                var match = name.includes(term);
                card.style.display = match ? 'flex' : 'none';
                if (match) found = true;
            });
        } else {
            cards.forEach(function(card) {
                var name = card.querySelector('.surah-name').textContent.toLowerCase();
                var match = parts.every(function(p) { return name.includes(p); });
                card.style.display = match ? 'flex' : 'none';
                if (match) found = true;
            });
        }
        var noResultMsg = document.getElementById('noSurahResult');
        if (!found && trimmed.length > 0) {
            if (!noResultMsg) {
                noResultMsg = document.createElement('div');
                noResultMsg.id = 'noSurahResult';
                noResultMsg.style.cssText = 'text-align:center;padding:2.5rem 0;color:var(--text-secondary);font-size:1rem;';
                noResultMsg.innerHTML = '🔍 لا توجد سور تطابق بحثك<br><span style="font-size:0.8rem;opacity:0.7;">جرب كلمات بحث مختلفة</span>';
                grid.appendChild(noResultMsg);
            }
        } else {
            if (noResultMsg) noResultMsg.remove();
        }
    }

    if (searchTargetReaders) searchTargetReaders.addEventListener('click', function() {
        searchTarget = 'readers';
        searchTargetReaders.classList.add('active');
        if (searchTargetSurahs) searchTargetSurahs.classList.remove('active');
        if (searchTargetAyahs) searchTargetAyahs.classList.remove('active');
        if (searchInput) searchInput.placeholder = 'ابحث عن قارئ...';
        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';
        if (searchClearBtn) searchClearBtn.classList.remove('visible');
        if (searchResultsInfo) searchResultsInfo.classList.remove('show');
        if (readerSearchResults) readerSearchResults.classList.remove('show');
        renderAllSurahs();
    });

    if (searchTargetSurahs) searchTargetSurahs.addEventListener('click', function() {
        searchTarget = 'surahs';
        searchTargetSurahs.classList.add('active');
        if (searchTargetReaders) searchTargetReaders.classList.remove('active');
        if (searchTargetAyahs) searchTargetAyahs.classList.remove('active');
        if (searchInput) searchInput.placeholder = 'ابحث عن سورة...';
        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';
        if (searchClearBtn) searchClearBtn.classList.remove('visible');
        if (searchResultsInfo) searchResultsInfo.classList.remove('show');
        if (readerSearchResults) readerSearchResults.classList.remove('show');
        renderAllSurahs();
    });

    if (searchTargetAyahs) {
        searchTargetAyahs.addEventListener('click', function() {
            searchTarget = 'ayahs';
            searchTargetAyahs.classList.add('active');
            if (searchTargetReaders) searchTargetReaders.classList.remove('active');
            if (searchTargetSurahs) searchTargetSurahs.classList.remove('active');
            if (searchInput) searchInput.placeholder = 'ابحث عن آية...';
            if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';
            if (searchClearBtn) searchClearBtn.classList.remove('visible');
            if (searchResultsInfo) searchResultsInfo.classList.remove('show');
            if (readerSearchResults) readerSearchResults.classList.remove('show');
            renderAllSurahs();
            loadQuranTexts();
        });
    }

    function updateSearchResultsInfo(filter) {
        var info = $('searchResultsInfo');
        if (!info) return;
        var q = (filter || '').trim();
        if (!q) { info.classList.remove('show'); return; }
        if (searchTarget === 'readers') {
            var count = readerEntries.filter(function(r) { return r.name.indexOf(q.toLowerCase()) !== -1; })
                .length;
            info.textContent = 'تم العثور على ' + count + ' قارئ';
            info.classList.add('show');
        } else if (searchTarget === 'surahs') {
            var parts = q.split(' ');
            var count2 = surahNames.filter(function(name) {
                return parts.every(function(p) { return name.indexOf(p) !== -1; });
            }).length;
            info.textContent = 'تم العثور على ' + count2 + ' سورة';
            info.classList.add('show');
        } else if (searchTarget === 'ayahs') {
            info.textContent = 'ابحث عن آية (حرفين على الأقل)';
            info.classList.add('show');
        }
    }

    function updateReaderSearchResults(filter) {
        var resultsContainer = document.getElementById('readerSearchResults');
        if (!resultsContainer) return;
        var q = (filter || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!q || searchTarget !== 'readers') {
            resultsContainer.classList.remove('show');
            return;
        }
        var filtered = readerEntries.filter(function(e) { return e.name.indexOf(q) !== -1; });
        if (filtered.length === 0) {
            resultsContainer.innerHTML =
                '<div class="reader-search-item" style="color:var(--text-secondary);text-align:center;padding:1.5rem 0;font-size:1rem;">🔍 لا توجد نتائج<br><span style="font-size:0.8rem;opacity:0.7;">جرب كلمات بحث مختلفة</span></div>';
        } else {
            resultsContainer.innerHTML = filtered.map(function(e) {
                return '<div class="reader-search-item" data-reader="' + e.id + '">' + e.name +
                '</div>';
            }).join('');
            resultsContainer.querySelectorAll('.reader-search-item[data-reader]').forEach(function(item) {
                item.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    selectReader(item.dataset.reader);
                    document.getElementById('searchMainInput').value = '';
                    resultsContainer.classList.remove('show');
                    document.getElementById('searchResultsInfo').classList.remove('show');
                    document.getElementById('searchClearBtn').classList.remove('visible');
                });
            });
        }
        resultsContainer.classList.add('show');
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        if (readerSearchResults && !e.target.closest('#searchWrapper')) readerSearchResults.classList.remove(
        'show');
    });

    var dropdownBtn = $('readerDropdownBtn');
    if (dropdownBtn) dropdownBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        var menu = $('readerDropdownMenu');
        if (menu) {
            menu.classList.toggle('show');
            dropdownBtn.classList.toggle('open');
            if (menu.classList.contains('show')) renderReaderList('');
        }
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', function() {
        var menu = $('readerDropdownMenu'),
            btn = $('readerDropdownBtn');
        if (menu) menu.classList.remove('show');
        if (btn) btn.classList.remove('open');
    });

    var readerCountEl = $('readerCountDisplay');
    if (readerCountEl) readerCountEl.textContent = readerEntries.length;

    var welcomeModal = $('welcomeModal'),
        welcomeEnter = $('welcomeEnter');
    if (welcomeModal && welcomeEnter) {
        welcomeEnter.addEventListener('click', function() {
            welcomeModal.classList.add('hidden');
            document.body.classList.remove('modal-open');
            toastInfo('⏳ جاري تحميل السور...');
            if (!surahAvailability[currentReader]) {
                surahAvailability[currentReader] = {};
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
                if (!surahAvailability[currentReader][i]) {
                    surahAvailability[currentReader][i] = 'pending';
                }
                updateSurahCardStatus(i);
            }
            startCheckingAvailability();
            loadQuranTexts();
            var showHelp = localStorage.getItem('showHelp') !== 'false';
            if (showHelp) {
                var helpModal = document.getElementById('helpModal');
                if (helpModal) {
                    openModalUniversal('helpModal');
                    var helpClose = document.getElementById('helpClose'),
                        helpDontShow = document.getElementById('helpDontShowAgain');
                    var closeHelp = function() {
                        closeModalUniversal('helpModal');
                        if (helpDontShow && helpDontShow.checked) localStorage.setItem('showHelp', 'false');
                        if (!document.querySelector('.surah-card')) {
                            renderAllSurahs();
                        }
                        checkLastSession();
                    };
                    helpClose.addEventListener('click', closeHelp);
                    helpModal.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) return;
                    });
                } else {
                    if (!document.querySelector('.surah-card')) {
                        renderAllSurahs();
                    }
                    checkLastSession();
                }
            } else {
                if (!document.querySelector('.surah-card')) {
                    renderAllSurahs();
                }
                checkLastSession();
            }
        });
        welcomeModal.classList.remove('hidden');
        document.body.classList.add('modal-open');
    }

    function getSessions() {
        try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('sessions') || '[]'); } catch (e) { return []; }
    }

    function saveSessions(sessions) { localStorage.setItem('sessions', JSON.stringify(sessions)); }

    function addSession(type, reader, surah, time) {
        var sessions = getSessions();
        var existing = sessions.findIndex(function(s) {
            return s.type === type && s.reader === reader && (type === 'reader' ? s.surah === surah : true);
        });
        if (existing !== -1) {
            sessions[existing].time = time || 0;
            sessions[existing].surah = surah;
            sessions[existing].timestamp = Date.now();
        } else {
            sessions.push({
                type: type,
                reader: reader || currentReader,
                surah: surah !== undefined ? surah : -1,
                time: time || (audio.currentTime || 0),
                timestamp: Date.now()
            });
        }
        saveSessions(sessions);
    }

    function removeSession(index) {
        var sessions = getSessions();
        sessions.splice(index, 1);
        saveSessions(sessions);
    }

    function checkLastSession() {
        var sessions = getSessions();
        if (sessions.length === 0) return;
        var now = Date.now();
        var validSessions = sessions.filter(function(s) { return (now - s.timestamp) / (1000 * 60 * 60) <= 48; });
        if (validSessions.length === 0) return;
        if (document.querySelector('.resume-overlay')) return;
        var html = '';
        validSessions.forEach(function(session, index) {
            var readerName = getReaderName(session.reader);
            var timeStr = formatTime(session.time || 0);
            if (session.type === 'surah') {
                var surahName = surahNames[session.surah] || 'غير معروف';
                html += '<div class="resume-item"><div><i data-icon="headphones"></i> استماع: سورة ' +
                    surahName + ' (' + readerName +
                    ')</div><div style="display:flex;gap:.5rem;justify-content:center;margin-top:.5rem;flex-wrap:wrap;"><button class="resume-btn" data-index="' +
                    index + '" data-action="play" data-reader="' + session.reader + '" data-surah="' +
                    session.surah + '" data-time="' + session.time + '">▶️ متابعة (' + timeStr +
                    ')</button><button class="resume-btn" data-index="' + index +
                    '" data-action="play" data-reader="' + session.reader + '" data-surah="' + session
                    .surah + '" data-time="0">🔄 إعادة</button><button class="resume-ignore-btn" data-index="' +
                    index + '">⏹️ تجاهل</button></div></div>';
            } else if (session.type === 'reader') {
                var surahName = surahNames[session.surah] || 'غير معروف';
                html += '<div class="resume-item"><div><i data-icon="book-open"></i> قراءة: سورة ' +
                    surahName +
                    '</div><div style="display:flex;gap:.5rem;justify-content:center;margin-top:.5rem;flex-wrap:wrap;"><button class="resume-btn" data-index="' +
                    index + '" data-action="read" data-surah="' + session.surah +
                    '">📖 فتح</button><button class="resume-ignore-btn" data-index="' + index +
                    '">⏹️ تجاهل</button></div></div>';
            }
        });
        if (!html) return;
        var overlay = document.createElement('div');
        overlay.className = 'resume-overlay';
        overlay.style.cssText =
            'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:999;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.7);backdrop-filter:blur(6px);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(6px);padding:1.25rem;';
        overlay.innerHTML =
            '<div style="background:var(--bg-card);border-radius:var(--radius);padding:calc(1.5rem*var(--display-scale));max-width:25rem;width:100%;box-shadow:0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.2);color:var(--text);text-align:center;max-height:80vh;overflow-y:auto;"><h3 style="text-align:center;font-size:calc(1.1rem*var(--text-scale));color:var(--gold-dark);margin-bottom:.75rem;"><i data-icon="history"></i> استئناف الجلسات السابقة</h3><div style="margin:1rem 0;">' +
            html +
            '</div><button class="btn-close-modal" id="sessionClose" style="width:100%;padding:.6rem 2rem;background:var(--gold);border:none;border-radius:2rem;color:#fff;font-family:var(--font-ui);font-weight:700;font-size:calc(.95rem*var(--text-scale));cursor:pointer;transition:all .15s ease;box-shadow:0 2px 12px rgba(201,168,76,.3);margin-top:.5rem;">إغلاق</button></div>';
        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        document.body.style.position = 'fixed';
        document.body.style.width = '100%';
        document.body.style.height = '100%';
        overlay.querySelectorAll('.resume-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
            btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                var action = this.dataset.action,
                    index = parseInt(this.dataset.index),
                    readerId = this.dataset.reader,
                    surah = parseInt(this.dataset.surah),
                    time = parseFloat(this.dataset.time) || 0;
                if (action === 'play') {
                    if (readerId) {
                        selectReader(readerId);
                        setTimeout(function() {
                            if (readerId === currentReader && surah === currentSurah)
                                currentSurah = -1;
                            playSurah(surah, time);
                        }, 300);
                    }
                    removeSession(index);
                } else if (action === 'read') {
                    openReader(surah);
                    removeSession(index);
                }
                var item = this.closest('.resume-item');
                if (item) item.remove();
                var remaining = overlay.querySelectorAll('.resume-item').length;
                if (remaining === 0) {
                    overlay.remove();
                    document.body.style.overflow = '';
                    document.body.style.position = '';
                    document.body.style.width = '';
                    document.body.style.height = '';
                }
            });
        });
        overlay.querySelectorAll('.resume-ignore-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
            btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                var index = parseInt(this.dataset.index);
                removeSession(index);
                var item = this.closest('.resume-item');
                if (item) item.remove();
                var remaining = overlay.querySelectorAll('.resume-item').length;
                if (remaining === 0) {
                    overlay.remove();
                    document.body.style.overflow = '';
                    document.body.style.position = '';
                    document.body.style.width = '';
                    document.body.style.height = '';
                }
            });
        });
        overlay.querySelector('#sessionClose').addEventListener('click', function() {
            overlay.remove();
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
            document.body.style.position = '';
            document.body.style.width = '';
            document.body.style.height = '';
        });
        applyIconsToAll();
    }

    function toggleSideMenu(open) {
        var overlay = $('sideMenuOverlay'),
            menu = $('sideMenu'),
            main = $('mainApp');
        if (!overlay || !menu) return;
        if (open) {
            overlay.classList.add('show');
            menu.classList.add('open');
            if (main) main.classList.add('blurred');
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
            pushWindow('sideMenu');
            history.pushState({ modal: 'sideMenu' }, '', window.location.href);
        } else {
            overlay.classList.remove('show');
            menu.classList.remove('open');
            if (main) main.classList.remove('blurred');
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
            windowStack = windowStack.filter(function(w) { return w !== 'sideMenu'; });
            if (history.state && history.state.modal === 'sideMenu') {
                history.replaceState({ modal: windowStack.length > 0 ? windowStack[windowStack.length - 1] : null }, '', window.location.href);
            }
        }
    }

    var menuToggle = $('menuToggle');
    if (menuToggle) menuToggle.addEventListener('click', function() { toggleSideMenu(true); });

    var sideMenuClose = $('sideMenuClose');
    if (sideMenuClose) sideMenuClose.addEventListener('click', function() { toggleSideMenu(false); });

    function showFavReadersModal() {
        var favList = readerEntries.filter(function(r) { return favReaders.indexOf(r.id) !== -1; });
        if (favList.length === 0) { toastInfo('لا يوجد قراء مفضلون'); return; }
        var overlay = document.createElement('div');
        overlay.className = 'tafsir-modal show';
        overlay.style.display = 'flex';
        overlay.style.alignItems = 'center';
        overlay.style.justifyContent = 'center';
        overlay.style.zIndex = '300';
        overlay.innerHTML =
            '<div class="tafsir-content">' +
            '<h3><i data-icon="star"></i> مفضلة القراء</h3>' +
            '<div class="modal-list">' +
            favList.map(function(r) {
                return '<div class="modal-item" data-id="' + r.id + '">' +
                       '<span class="item-name">' + r.name + '</span>' +
                       '<span class="item-action"><i data-icon="chevron-left"></i></span>' +
                       '</div>';
            }).join('') +
            '</div>' +
            '<button class="btn-close-modal" id="favReadersClose">' +
            '<i data-icon="times"></i> إغلاق</button>' +
            '</div>';
        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.add('blurred');
        pushWindow('favReadersModal');
        function closeFavReaders() {
            overlay.remove();
            document.body.style.overflow = '';
            document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.remove('blurred');
            windowStack = windowStack.filter(function(w) { return w !== 'favReadersModal'; });
        }
        overlay.querySelectorAll('.modal-item').forEach(function(item) {
            item.addEventListener('click', function() {
                selectReader(this.dataset.id);
                closeFavReaders();
            });
        });
        overlay.querySelector('#favReadersClose').addEventListener('click', closeFavReaders);
        overlay.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            if (e.target === e.currentTarget) closeFavReaders();
        });
        applyIconsToAll();
    }

    }

            toggleSideMenu(false);

                return;

            if (surahAvailability[readerId]) {

            document.getElementById('cancelVerificationBtn').style.display = 'inline-flex';

            document.getElementById('manualVerifyBtn').style.display = 'none';
            startCheckingAvailability();
            toastInfo('جاري التحقق من السور...');
        });

        toggleSideMenu(false);
        var shareText =
            '📱 اسم التطبيق: القرآن الكريم\n\n👨‍💻 اسم المطور: منجد أمهز\n\n📖 الوصف: استمع وتدبّر القرآن الكريم بأصوات أكثر من ١٠٠ قارئ من مختلف أنحاء العالم. إمكانية الاستماع المباشر للسور، راديو حي، تنزيل السور، قراءة الآيات مع التفسير، ومفضلة للسور والقراء.\n\n🔗 الرابط: https://mamhaz.github.io/QuranKarim';
        if (navigator.share) {
            navigator.share({ title: 'القرآن الكريم - منجد أمهز', text: shareText }).catch(function() {});
        } else {
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareText).then(function() {
                toastSuccess('تم نسخ معلومات التطبيق للمشاركة');
            }).catch(function() { toastError('تعذر المشاركة'); });
        }
    });

                if (localStorage.getItem('showHelp') === 'false') {
                    dontShowContainer.style.display = 'none';

                }
            }
        });
    }

        helpClose.addEventListener('click', function() {
            closeModalUniversal('helpModal');

            if (helpDontShow && helpDontShow.checked) localStorage.setItem('showHelp', 'false');
        });
    }

    var resetActions = { readers: false, surahs: false, downloads: false },
        currentCaptcha = '';

    function generateRandomCode() {
        var length = Math.random() > 0.5 ? 4 : 5;
        var code = '';
        for (var i = 0; i < length; i++) code += Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);
        return code;
    }

    function openResetModal(modalId) {
        var modal = document.getElementById(modalId);
        if (!modal) return;
        modal.style.display = 'flex';
        modal.classList.add('show');
        lockBodyScroll();
        document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.add('blurred');
        pushWindow(modalId);
        history.pushState({ modal: modalId }, '', window.location.href);
    }

    function closeResetModal(modalId) {
        var modal = document.getElementById(modalId);
        if (!modal) return;
        modal.style.display = 'none';
        modal.classList.remove('show');
        windowStack = windowStack.filter(function(w) { return w !== modalId; });
        if (windowStack.length === 0) {
            unlockBodyScroll();
            document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.remove('blurred');
        }
        if (history.state && history.state.modal === modalId) {
            history.replaceState({ modal: windowStack.length > 0 ? windowStack[windowStack.length - 1] : null }, '', window.location.href);
        }
    }

    async function updateDownloadSize() {
        var count = 0,
            totalSize = 0;
        for (var readerId in downloaded) {
            for (var surah in downloaded[readerId]) {
                if (downloaded[readerId][surah] && downloaded[readerId][surah].downloaded) {
                    count++;
                    if (downloaded[readerId][surah].size) totalSize += downloaded[readerId][surah].size;
                }
            }
        }
        if (count > 0 && totalSize === 0) {
            try {
                var cache = await caches.open('quran-audio');
                var keys = await cache.keys();
                for (var request of keys) {
                    var response = await cache.match(request);
                    if (response) {
                        var blob = await response.blob();
                        totalSize += blob.size;
                    }
                }
            } catch (e) {}
        }
        var label = document.getElementById('resetDownloadsLabel');
        if (label) {
            if (count === 0) label.textContent = 'مسح السور المُنزّلة (0 سورة)';
            else if (totalSize > 0) label.textContent = 'مسح السور المُنزّلة (' + count + ' سورة - ' + (
                totalSize / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1) + ' م.ب)';
            else label.textContent = 'مسح السور المُنزّلة (' + count + ' سورة - حجم غير معروف)';
        }
    }

    function showResetModal() {
        updateDownloadSize();
        document.getElementById('resetFavReaders').checked = true;
        document.getElementById('resetFavSurahs').checked = true;
        document.getElementById('resetDownloads').checked = true;
        openResetModal('resetModal');
    }

    function hideResetModal() { closeResetModal('resetModal'); }

    function showVerificationModal() {
        hideResetModal();
        currentCaptcha = generateRandomCode();
        document.getElementById('captchaCode').textContent = currentCaptcha;
        document.getElementById('captchaInput').value = '';
        document.getElementById('verifyError').style.display = 'none';
        openResetModal('verifyModal');
        setTimeout(function() { document.getElementById('captchaInput').focus(); }, 300);
    }

    function cancelVerification() { closeResetModal('verifyModal');
        showResetModal(); }

    function executeReset() {
        resetActions.readers = document.getElementById('resetFavReaders').checked;
        resetActions.surahs = document.getElementById('resetFavSurahs').checked;
        resetActions.downloads = document.getElementById('resetDownloads').checked;
        if (!resetActions.readers && !resetActions.surahs && !resetActions.downloads) {
            toastWarning('يرجى اختيار إجراء واحد على الأقل');
            return;
        }
        showVerificationModal();
    }

    var savedScrollPos = 0;

    function lockBodyScroll() {
        savedScrollPos = window.scrollY || window.pageYOffset;
        document.body.style.top = '-' + savedScrollPos + 'px';
        document.body.classList.add('reset-modal-open');
    }

    function unlockBodyScroll() {
        document.body.classList.remove('reset-modal-open');
        document.body.style.top = '';
        window.scrollTo(0, savedScrollPos);
    }

    function verifyAndExecute() {
        var input = document.getElementById('captchaInput').value.trim();
        if (input === currentCaptcha) {
            closeResetModal('verifyModal');
            if (resetActions.readers) {
                var defaultFavs = ["abdulbaset_murattal", "abdulbaset_mujawwad", "minsh_murattal",
                    "minsh_mujawwad", "maher_murattal"
                ];
                favReaders = defaultFavs.filter(function(id) { return getReader(id); });
                localStorage.setItem('favReaders', JSON.stringify(favReaders));
                toastSuccess('تم إعادة تعيين تفضيل القراء إلى الافتراضي');
            }
            if (resetActions.surahs) {
                favSurahs = [];
                toastSuccess('تم إعادة تعيين تفضيل السور');
            }
            if (resetActions.downloads) {
                downloaded = {};
                caches.open('quran-audio').then(function(cache) {
                    cache.keys().then(function(keys) { keys.forEach(function(k) { cache.delete(k); }); });
                });
                toastSuccess('تم مسح جميع السور المُنزّلة');
            }
            debouncedSaveState();
            renderReaderList();
            renderAllSurahs();
            updateFavReaderStar();
            toastSuccess('تم إعادة تعيين التطبيق بنجاح');
        } else {
            document.getElementById('verifyError').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('captchaInput').value = '';
            document.getElementById('captchaInput').focus();
            currentCaptcha = generateRandomCode();
            document.getElementById('captchaCode').textContent = currentCaptcha;
            setTimeout(function() { document.getElementById('verifyError').style.display = 'none'; }, 3000);
        }
    }

    var resetCancel = document.getElementById('resetCancel');
    if (resetCancel) resetCancel.addEventListener('click', hideResetModal);

    var resetConfirm = document.getElementById('resetConfirm');
    if (resetConfirm) resetConfirm.addEventListener('click', executeReset);

    var verifyCancelBtn = document.getElementById('verifyCancel');
    if (verifyCancelBtn) verifyCancelBtn.addEventListener('click', cancelVerification);

    var verifyConfirmBtn = document.getElementById('verifyConfirm');
    if (verifyConfirmBtn) verifyConfirmBtn.addEventListener('click', verifyAndExecute);

    var captchaInput = document.getElementById('captchaInput');
    if (captchaInput) {
        captchaInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
            this.value = this.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
        });
        captchaInput.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                e.preventDefault();
                verifyAndExecute();
            }
        });
    }

    document.getElementById('dlConfirmOk').addEventListener('click', function() {
        var isMulti = document.getElementById('dlConfirmMulti').checked;
        if (isMulti) {
            closeModalUniversal('downloadConfirmModal');
            showSurahSelectionModal(confirmIdx);
        } else {
            const downloadId = `${currentReader}_${confirmIdx}`;
            if (activeDownloads[downloadId]) {
                toastWarning('⚠️ التحميل جارٍ بالفعل لهذه السورة');
                return;
            }
            closeModalUniversal('downloadConfirmModal');
            startNewDownload(currentReader, confirmIdx);
        }
    });

    document.getElementById('dlConfirmCancel').addEventListener('click', function() {
        closeModalUniversal('downloadConfirmModal');
    });

    document.getElementById('dlConfirmMulti').addEventListener('change', function() {
        document.getElementById('dlConfirmBtnText').textContent = this.checked ? 'اختيار السور' : 'تنزيل';
    });

    var selSelectAll = document.getElementById('selSelectAll'),
        selDeselectAll = document.getElementById('selDeselectAll'),
        selCancel = document.getElementById('selCancel'),
        selDownload = document.getElementById('selDownload'),
        dlProgressCancel = document.getElementById('dlProgressCancel'),
        dlProgressBack = document.getElementById('dlProgressBack');

    if (selSelectAll) selSelectAll.addEventListener('click', function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('#selectionGrid input[type="checkbox"]:not(:disabled)').forEach(function(cb) {
            cb.checked = true;
        });
        updateSelectionStats();
    });

    if (selDeselectAll) selDeselectAll.addEventListener('click', function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('#selectionGrid input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(function(cb) { cb.checked =
                false; });
        updateSelectionStats();
    });

    if (selCancel) selCancel.addEventListener('click', function() {
        closeModalUniversal('surahSelectionModal');
        showDownloadConfirm(confirmIdx);
    });

    if (selDownload) selDownload.addEventListener('click', downloadSelectedSurahs);

    if (dlProgressCancel) dlProgressCancel.addEventListener('click', function() {
        downloadCancel = true;
        abortCurrentDownload();
        toastInfo('تم إيقاف التنزيل');
        isDownloadingMultiple = false;
        closeModalUniversal('downloadProgressOverlay');
        unlockBodyScroll();
        document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.remove('blurred');
    });

    if (dlProgressBack) dlProgressBack.addEventListener('click', function() {
        closeModalUniversal('downloadProgressOverlay');
        unlockBodyScroll();
        document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.remove('blurred');
        toastInfo('التنزيل مستمر في الخلفية');
    });

    async function getCachedUrl(url) {
        try {
            var cache = await caches.open('quran-audio');
            var cached = await cache.match(url);
            if (cached) return URL.createObjectURL(await cached.blob());
        } catch (e) {}
        return null;
    }

    var currentFilter = 'all';

    function getDownloadStats() {
        var readerId = currentReader;
        var count = 0;
        var totalSize = 0;
        if (downloaded[readerId]) {
            for (var idx in downloaded[readerId]) {
                if (downloaded[readerId][idx].downloaded) {
                    count++;
                    if (downloaded[readerId][idx].size) totalSize += downloaded[readerId][idx].size;
                }
            }
        }
        return { count: count, size: totalSize };
    }

    function filterSurahs(filter) {
    currentFilter = filter;
    var cards = document.querySelectorAll('.surah-card');
    var readerId = currentReader;
    var count = 0;
    
    document.querySelectorAll('.filter-option').forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.filter === filter);
    });
    
    cards.forEach(function(card) {
        var idx = parseInt(card.dataset.idx);
        var isDl = downloaded[readerId] && downloaded[readerId][idx] && downloaded[readerId][idx].downloaded;
        var status = surahAvailability[readerId]?.[idx];
        var show = true;
        switch(filter) {
            case 'all': show = true; break;
            case 'available': show = (status === 'available' || status === 'cached'); break;
            case 'unavailable': show = (status === 'unavailable'); break;
            case 'downloaded': show = isDl; break;
            case 'notdownloaded': show = !isDl; break;
        }
        card.style.display = show ? 'flex' : 'none';
        if (show) count++;
    });
    
    var grid = document.getElementById('surahGrid');
    var noResultMsg = document.getElementById('noFilterResult');
    var filterName = getFilterName(filter);
    var filterBtn = document.getElementById('filterToggleBtn');
    
    if (count === 0 && filter !== 'all' && grid) {
        if (filterBtn) {
            filterBtn.style.animation = 'pulse-filter 1.5s ease-in-out 3';
            filterBtn.style.borderColor = 'var(--gold)';
            setTimeout(() => {
                filterBtn.style.animation = '';
                filterBtn.style.borderColor = '';
            }, 4500);
        }
        
        if (!noResultMsg) {
            noResultMsg = document.createElement('div');
            noResultMsg.id = 'noFilterResult';
            noResultMsg.style.cssText = 'text-align:center;padding:2.5rem 0;color:var(--text-secondary);';
            noResultMsg.innerHTML = 
                '🔍 لا توجد سور بفلتر "<strong style="color:var(--gold-dark);">' + filterName + '</strong>"<br>' +
                '<span style="font-size:0.8rem;opacity:0.7;">' +
                '💡 اضغط على <strong style="color:var(--gold);cursor:pointer;" onclick="document.getElementById(\'filterToggleBtn\').click()">' +
                '<i class="fas fa-filter" style="font-size:0.9rem;margin:0 0.2rem;"></i> أيقونة الفلتر</strong> لتغيير الفلتر أو اختيار "<strong style="color:var(--gold);cursor:pointer;" onclick="resetFilter()">الكل</strong>"' +
                '</span>';
            grid.appendChild(noResultMsg);
        } else {
            noResultMsg.innerHTML = 
                '🔍 لا توجد سور بفلتر "<strong style="color:var(--gold-dark);">' + filterName + '</strong>"<br>' +
                '<span style="font-size:0.8rem;opacity:0.7;">' +
                '💡 اضغط على <strong style="color:var(--gold);cursor:pointer;" onclick="document.getElementById(\'filterToggleBtn\').click()">' +
                '<i class="fas fa-filter" style="font-size:0.9rem;margin:0 0.2rem;"></i> أيقونة الفلتر</strong> لتغيير الفلتر أو اختيار "<strong style="color:var(--gold);cursor:pointer;" onclick="resetFilter()">الكل</strong>"' +
                '</span>';
        }
        noResultMsg.style.display = 'block';
    } else {
        if (noResultMsg) {
            noResultMsg.remove();
        }
    }
    
    var countEl = document.getElementById('filterCount');
    if (!countEl) {
        var info = document.getElementById('searchResultsInfo');
        if (info) {
            var span = document.createElement('span');
            span.id = 'filterCount';
            span.style.cssText = 'margin-right:0.5rem;font-size:calc(.7rem*var(--text-scale));color:var(--text-secondary);';
            info.appendChild(span);
            countEl = span;
        }
    }
    if (countEl) {
        var total = cards.length;
        countEl.textContent = ' (' + count + '/' + total + ')';
    }
    
    var explanation = document.getElementById('filterExplanation');
    if (!explanation) return;
    
    var filterNames = {
        'all': '📋 الكل',
        'available': '✅ المتوفرة',
        'unavailable': '❌ غير المتوفرة',
        'downloaded': '⬇️ المُنزّلة',
        'notdownloaded': '📤 غير المُنزّلة'
    };
    
    var descriptions = {
        'all': 'يعرض جميع السور الـ 114 بغض النظر عن حالتها.',
        'available': 'يعرض السور المتوفرة للاستماع مباشرة من الإنترنت للقارئ الحالي.',
        'unavailable': 'يعرض السور غير المتوفرة للقارئ الحالي (غير موجودة على الخادم).',
        'downloaded': 'يعرض السور التي تم تنزيلها مسبقاً على الجهاز (للاستماع دون إنترنت).',
        'notdownloaded': 'يعرض السور التي لم يتم تنزيلها بعد (تحتاج اتصالاً بالإنترنت).'
    };
    
    var repeatBehavior = {
        'all': '🔄 <strong>التكرار:</strong> يشغل جميع السور الـ 114 بالتسلسل (1 → 2 → 3 → ... → 114 → 1).',
        'available': '🔄 <strong>التكرار:</strong> يشغل السور المتوفرة فقط، ويتجاوز غير المتوفرة (مثال: 2 → 4 → 5 → 7).',
        'unavailable': '🔄 <strong>التكرار:</strong> لا توجد سور للتشغيل (القائمة فارغة).',
        'downloaded': '🔄 <strong>التكرار:</strong> يشغل السور المُنزّلة فقط، ويتجاوز غير المُنزّلة (مثال: 2 → 4 → 5 → 7).',
        'notdownloaded': '🔄 <strong>التكرار:</strong> لا توجد سور للتشغيل (القائمة فارغة).'
    };
    
    var repeatWarning = {
        'all': '',
        'available': '⚠️ إذا كان هناك سور غير متوفرة، سيتم تخطيها تلقائياً.',
        'unavailable': '⚠️ هذا الفلتر يعرض السور غير المتوفرة فقط، لذا لا يمكن تشغيلها.',
        'downloaded': '⚠️ إذا كان هناك سور غير مُنزّلة، سيتم تخطيها تلقائياً.',
        'notdownloaded': '⚠️ هذا الفلتر يعرض السور غير المُنزّلة فقط، لذا لا يمكن تشغيلها.'
    };
    
    var currentDesc = descriptions[filter] || '';
    var currentRepeat = repeatBehavior[filter] || '';
    var currentWarning = repeatWarning[filter] || '';
    var visibleCount = count;
    var totalSurahs = cards.length;
    
    explanation.innerHTML = 
    '<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:0.2rem;">' +
    '<div style="display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:0.3rem;">' +
    '<span style="font-size:0.9rem;flex-shrink:0;">ℹ️</span>' +
    '<div style="flex:1;">' +
    '<div><strong style="color:var(--gold-dark);font-size:0.8rem;">' + filterNames[filter] + '</strong> ' + 
    '<span style="font-size:0.7rem;">' + currentDesc + '</span>' +
    ' <span class="filter-count" style="background:var(--bg);padding:0.05rem 0.5rem;border-radius:1rem;font-size:0.6rem;color:var(--text-secondary);">' + visibleCount + '/' + totalSurahs + '</span></div>' +
    '<div style="margin-top:0.1rem;font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text-secondary);">' + currentRepeat + '</div>' +
    (currentWarning ? '<div class="filter-warning" style="margin-top:0.05rem;font-size:0.65rem;color:#e67e22;">' + currentWarning + '</div>' : '') +
    '</div>' +
    '</div>' +
    '<div class="filter-footer" style="font-size:0.6rem;color:var(--text-secondary);border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:0.15rem;margin-top:0.15rem;text-align:center;">' +
    '💡 عند تفعيل <strong>"تكرار الكل"</strong>، سيتنقل بين السور المرئية فقط' +
    '</div>' +
    '</div>';
    
    explanation.style.display = 'block';
    explanation.style.animation = 'fadeIn 0.3s ease';
    
    clearTimeout(explanation._hideTimer);
    explanation._hideTimer = setTimeout(function() {
        explanation.style.display = 'none';
    }, 10000);
}

    document.getElementById('filterToggleBtn').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        document.getElementById('filterDropdown').classList.toggle('show');
        this.classList.toggle('open');
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.filter-option').forEach(function(btn) {
        btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            var filter = this.dataset.filter;
            document.querySelectorAll('.filter-option').forEach(function(b) {
                b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.filter === filter);
            });
            document.getElementById('filterDropdown').classList.remove('show');
            document.getElementById('filterToggleBtn').classList.remove('open');
            filterSurahs(filter);
        });
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        if (!e.target.closest('.filter-wrapper')) {
            document.getElementById('filterDropdown').classList.remove('show');
            document.getElementById('filterToggleBtn').classList.remove('open');
        }
        if (!e.target.closest('.stats-btn')) {
            document.getElementById('statsTooltip').classList.remove('show');
        }
    });

    document.getElementById('statsBtn').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        var tooltip = document.getElementById('statsTooltip');
        if (tooltip.classList.contains('show')) {
            tooltip.classList.remove('show');
            if (history.state && history.state.modal === 'statsTooltip') {
                history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.href);
            }
        } else {
            tooltip.classList.add('show');
            history.pushState({ modal: 'statsTooltip' }, '', window.location.href);
        }
        updateStatsTooltip();
    });

    function updateStatsTooltip() {
        var done = 0;
        var total = 0;
        var available = 0;
        var unavailable = 0;
        var failed = 0;
        var unavailableSurahs = [];
        var failedSurahs = [];
        var readerId = currentReader;
        if (surahAvailability[readerId]) {
            for (var i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
                if (surahAvailability[readerId][i] !== undefined) {
                    done++;
                    if (surahAvailability[readerId][i] === 'available' || surahAvailability[readerId][i] === 'cached') available++;
                    else if (surahAvailability[readerId][i] === 'unavailable') {
                        unavailable++;
                        unavailableSurahs.push(i);
                    } else if (surahAvailability[readerId][i] === 'failed') {
                        failed++;
                        failedSurahs.push(i);
                    }
                }
                total++;
            }
        }
        var tooltip = document.getElementById('statsTooltip');
        if (tooltip) {
            var downloadStats = getDownloadStats();
            var sizeText = downloadStats.size > 0 ? formatSize(downloadStats.size) : '0';
            var unavailableHtml = '';
            if (unavailableSurahs.length > 0) {
                var surahList = unavailableSurahs.map(function(idx) {
                    return '<div class="surah-item"><span>' + (idx + 1) + '</span><span>' + surahNames[idx] + '</span></div>';
                }).join('');
                unavailableHtml = '<div class="unavailable-list">❌ غير متوفرة: ' + surahList + '</div>';
            }
            var failedHtml = '';
            if (failedSurahs.length > 0) {
                var failedList = failedSurahs.map(function(idx) {
                    return '<div class="surah-item"><span>' + (idx + 1) + '</span><span>' + surahNames[idx] + '</span></div>';
                }).join('');
                failedHtml = '<div class="unavailable-list" style="border-right-color:#f39c12;color:#f39c12;">⚠️ فشل التحقق: ' + failedList + '</div>';
            }
            tooltip.innerHTML = 
                '<div class="stat-item"><span class="label">📦 متوفر</span><span class="value">' + available + '</span></div>' +
                '<div class="stat-item"><span class="label">❌ غير متوفر</span><span class="value">' + unavailable + '</span></div>' +
                '<div class="stat-item"><span class="label">⚠️ فشل التحقق</span><span class="value">' + failed + '</span></div>' +
                (unavailableHtml) +
                (failedHtml) +
                '<div class="stat-item"><span class="label">⬇️ مُنزّل</span><span class="value">' + downloadStats.count + ' (' + sizeText + ')</span></div>' +
                '<button class="btn-gold-small" id="statsCloseBtn">👍 موافق</button>';
            var closeBtn = document.getElementById('statsCloseBtn');
            if (closeBtn) {
                closeBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                    document.getElementById('statsTooltip').classList.remove('show');
                    if (history.state && history.state.modal === 'statsTooltip') {
                        history.replaceState({}, '', window.location.href);
                    }
                });
            }
        }
    }

    async function renderAllSurahs(force = false) {
        if (isRendering) {
            return;
        }
        if (!force && !isInitialRender && document.querySelector('.surah-card')) {
            return;
        }
        isRendering = true;
        isInitialRender = false;
        try {
            var grid = $('surahGrid');
            if (!grid) {
                isRendering = false;
                return;
            }
            grid.innerHTML = '';
            grid.removeEventListener('click', handleSurahAction);
            var readerId = currentReader;
            if (!surahAvailability[readerId]) surahAvailability[readerId] = {};
            for (var i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
                if (!surahAvailability[readerId][i]) {
                    surahAvailability[readerId][i] = 'pending';
                }
                const isInCache = await syncSurahWithCache(readerId, i);
                var card = document.createElement('div');
                card.className = 'surah-card';
                card.dataset.idx = i;
                var isFav = favSurahs.indexOf(i) !== -1;
                var isDl = isInCache;
                var isPartial = downloadProgress[readerId] && downloadProgress[readerId][i] !== undefined &&
                    downloadProgress[readerId][i] < 100;
                var status = surahAvailability[readerId] && surahAvailability[readerId][i];
                var btnHtml = '';
                if (isPartial) {
                    var pct = Math.round(downloadProgress[readerId][i]);
                    btnHtml =
                        '<button class="download-btn downloading" data-idx="' + i +
                        '" data-action="resume"><svg class="progress-ring" viewBox="0 0 40 40"><circle class="bg" cx="20" cy="20" r="16"/><circle class="fg" cx="20" cy="20" r="16" stroke-dasharray="100.53" stroke-dashoffset="' +
                        (100.53 * (1 - pct / 100)) +
                        '"/></svg><span class="progress-text">' + pct +
                        '%</span><span class="cancel-icon" data-idx="' + i + '">×</span></button>';
                } else if (isDl) {
                    btnHtml =
                        '<button class="download-btn downloaded" data-idx="' + i +
                        '" title="حذف التنزيل"><i data-icon="check-circle"></i></button>';
                } else {
                    var btnClass = 'download-btn';
                    if (status === 'unavailable') btnClass += ' unavailable not-exist';
                    else if (status === 'failed') btnClass += ' verify-failed';
                    else if (status === 'pending') btnClass += ' verify-pending';
                    btnHtml = '<button class="' + btnClass + '" data-idx="' + i +
                        '"><i data-icon="download"></i></button>';
                }
                var showStatusRow = (status === 'unavailable' || status === 'failed' || status === 'pending');
                var showRetryBtn = (status === 'failed');
                card.innerHTML =
                    '<div class="surah-main-row"><span class="surah-number">' + (i + 1) +
                    '</span><span class="surah-name">' + surahNames[i] +
                    '</span><div class="surah-actions"><button class="play-btn" data-idx="' + i +
                    '"><i data-icon="play"></i></button><button class="read-btn" data-idx="' + i +
                    '"><i data-icon="book"></i></button><button class="fav-btn ' + (isFav ? 'active' : '') +
                    '" data-idx="' + i + '"><i data-icon="star"></i></button>' + btnHtml +
                    '</div></div><div class="surah-status-row" style="' + (showStatusRow ? '' : 'display:none;') +
                    '"><span class="checking-spinner"></span><span class="unavailability-badge">❌ غير موجودة في المصدر</span><span class="warning-badge">' + (networkOnline ? '⚠️ فشل التحقق (ضعف الاتصال)' : '⚠️ لا يوجد اتصال') + '</span>' +
                    (showRetryBtn ?
                        '<button class="retry-check-btn show" data-idx="' + i +
                        '" title="إعادة التحقق"><i data-icon="sync"></i> ' + (networkOnline ? 'إعادة المحاولة' : '📡 انتظر الاتصال') + '</button>' : '') +
                    '</div>';
                grid.appendChild(card);
            }
            grid.addEventListener('click', handleSurahAction);
            for (var j = 0; j < TOTAL_SURAHS; j++) await updateSurahCardStatus(j);
            startCheckingAvailability();
            applyIconsToAll();
            filterSurahs(currentFilter);
        } catch (error) {
        } finally {
            isRendering = false;
        }
    }

    async function handleSurahAction(e) {
        var target = e.target.closest('button');
        if (!target) return;
        var card = target.closest('.surah-card');
        if (!card) return;
        var idx = parseInt(card.dataset.idx);
        if (isNaN(idx)) return;
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (target.classList.contains('play-btn')) {
            playSurah(idx, 0);
        } else if (target.classList.contains('read-btn')) {
            openReader(idx);
        } else if (target.classList.contains('fav-btn')) {
            toggleFavSurah(idx);
        } else if (target.classList.contains('download-btn')) {
            var isDl = downloaded[currentReader] && downloaded[currentReader][idx] && downloaded[currentReader][idx].downloaded;
            if (isDl) {
                showDeleteConfirm(idx);
                return;
            }
            if (idx < 0 || idx > 113) {
                toastError('رقم سورة غير صحيح');
                return;
            }
            const downloadId = `${currentReader}_${idx}`;
            if (activeDownloads[downloadId]) {
                showAlertHtml(
                    `⏳ التحميل جارٍ بالفعل لسورة <strong>"${surahNames[idx]}"</strong>.<br>هل تريد إلغاء التحميل؟`,
                    function() {
                        const downloadInfo = activeDownloads[downloadId];
                        if (downloadInfo && downloadInfo.xhr) {
                            downloadInfo.xhr.abort();
                            toastInfo('تم إلغاء تحميل "' + surahNames[idx] + '"');
                            if (downloadProgress[currentReader]) {
                                delete downloadProgress[currentReader][idx];
                            }
                            updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
                        }
                    }
                );
                return;
            }
            showDownloadConfirm(idx);
        } else if (target.classList.contains('retry-check-btn')) {
            var idx = parseInt(target.dataset.idx);
            if (!isNaN(idx)) {
                if (!networkOnline) {
                    toastWarning('لا يوجد اتصال بالإنترنت');
                    return;
                }
                if (surahAvailability[currentReader]) {
                    delete surahAvailability[currentReader][idx];
                }
                toastInfo('جاري إعادة التحقق...');
                target.classList.add('checking');
                checkSurahAvailability(currentReader, idx);
                var checkInterval = setInterval(function() {
                    var newStatus = surahAvailability[currentReader] && surahAvailability[
                    currentReader][idx];
                    if (newStatus && newStatus !== 'pending') {
                        clearInterval(checkInterval);
                        target.classList.remove('checking');
                        if (newStatus === 'available' || newStatus === 'cached') {
                            toastSuccess('✅ السورة متوفرة');
                        } else if (newStatus === 'unavailable') {
                            toastWarning('❌ السورة غير موجودة في المصدر');
                        } else if (newStatus === 'failed') {
                            toastError('⚠️ فشل التحقق، تأكد من اتصالك بالإنترنت');
                        }
                    }
                }, 500);
                setTimeout(function() {
                    clearInterval(checkInterval);
                    target.classList.remove('checking');
                    var finalStatus = surahAvailability[currentReader] && surahAvailability[
                        currentReader][idx];
                    if (!finalStatus || finalStatus === 'pending') {
                        toastWarning('تعذر التحقق، تأكد من اتصالك بالإنترنت');
                    }
                }, 15000);
            }
        }
    }

    function playSurah(idx, startTime) {
        startTime = startTime || 0;
        var readerId = currentReader;
        if (idx < 0 || idx > 113) { toastError('رقم سورة غير صحيح'); return; }
        var status = surahAvailability[readerId] && surahAvailability[readerId][idx];
        var url = getReaderUrl(readerId, idx);
        
        caches.open('quran-audio').then(function(cache) {
            cache.match(url).then(function(response) {
                if (response) {
                    playSurahWithRetry(idx, 0, startTime);
                    return;
                }
                if (status === 'unavailable' && repeatMode === 'all') {
                    toastWarning('هذه السورة غير متوفرة لهذا القارئ');
                    var visibleSurahs = [];
                    document.querySelectorAll('.surah-card').forEach(function(card) {
                        if (card.style.display !== 'none') {
                            var i = parseInt(card.dataset.idx);
                            if (!isNaN(i)) visibleSurahs.push(i);
                        }
                    });
                    var currentIndex = visibleSurahs.indexOf(idx);
                    if (currentIndex !== -1 && visibleSurahs.length > 1) {
                        var nextIdx = (currentIndex + 1) % visibleSurahs.length;
                        if (nextIdx !== currentIndex) {
                            setTimeout(function() { playSurah(visibleSurahs[nextIdx], 0); }, 300);
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                    return;
                }
                if (status === 'failed') {
                    toastInfo('فشل التحقق سابقاً، جاري المحاولة...');
                }
                playSurahWithRetry(idx, 0, startTime);
            });
        });
    }

    function toggleFavSurah(idx) {
        var i = favSurahs.indexOf(idx);
        if (i >= 0) {
            favSurahs.splice(i, 1);
            toastSuccess('تم إزالة من المفضلة');
        } else {
            favSurahs.push(idx);
            toastSuccess('تمت الإضافة إلى المفضلة');
        }
        debouncedSaveState();
        renderAllSurahs();
    }

    function startCheckingAvailability() {
        verifyCancelFlag = false;
        var readerId = currentReader;
        if (!surahAvailability[readerId]) surahAvailability[readerId] = {};
        for (var i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
            if (downloaded[readerId] && downloaded[readerId][i] && downloaded[readerId][i].downloaded) {
                surahAvailability[readerId][i] = 'cached';
                updateSurahCardStatus(i);
            } else if (!surahAvailability[readerId][i]) {
                surahAvailability[readerId][i] = 'pending';
                updateSurahCardStatus(i);
            }
        }
        for (var i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
            if (!downloaded[readerId] || !downloaded[readerId][i] || !downloaded[readerId][i].downloaded) {
                checkSurahAvailability(readerId, i);
            }
        }
    }

    let verifyCancelFlag = false;
    let verifyAbortControllers = {};
    let verifyDone = 0;
    let verifyTotal = 0;

    function showVerifyProgress() {
        const bar = document.getElementById('verifyProgressBar');
        if (bar) bar.style.display = 'flex';
        verifyCancelFlag = false;
        const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('cancelVerificationBtn');
        const retryBtn = document.getElementById('retryFailedBtn');
        const manualBtn = document.getElementById('manualVerifyBtn');
        if (cancelBtn) cancelBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
        if (retryBtn) retryBtn.style.display = 'none';
        if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
    }

    function hideVerifyProgress() {
        const bar = document.getElementById('verifyProgressBar');
        if (!bar) return;
        const status = document.getElementById('verifyProgressStatus');
        const count = document.getElementById('verifyProgressCount');
        const fill = document.getElementById('verifyProgressFill');
        const retryBtn = document.getElementById('retryFailedBtn');
        const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('cancelVerificationBtn');
        const manualBtn = document.getElementById('manualVerifyBtn');
        if (verifyCancelFlag) {
            if (status) status.textContent = '⏸️ تم إلغاء التحقق';
            if (count) count.textContent = '';
            if (fill) fill.style.width = '0%';
            if (cancelBtn) cancelBtn.style.display = 'none';
            if (retryBtn) {
                retryBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                retryBtn.textContent = '🔄 إعادة التحقق';
                retryBtn.onclick = function() {
                    if (!networkOnline) {
                        toastWarning('لا يوجد اتصال بالإنترنت');
                        return;
                    }
                    verifyCancelFlag = false;
                    cancelBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                    retryBtn.style.display = 'none';
                    if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
                    startCheckingAvailability();
                };
            }
            if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
            bar.style.display = 'flex';
            return;
        }
        if (!networkOnline) {
            if (status) status.textContent = '⚠️ غير متصل - انتظر عودة الإنترنت';
            if (count) count.textContent = '';
            if (fill) fill.style.width = '0%';
            if (cancelBtn) cancelBtn.style.display = 'none';
            if (retryBtn) {
                retryBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                retryBtn.textContent = '🔄 إعادة المحاولة';
                retryBtn.onclick = function() {
                    if (networkOnline) {
                        verifyCancelFlag = false;
                        cancelBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                        retryBtn.style.display = 'none';
                        if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
                        startCheckingAvailability();
                    } else {
                        toastWarning('لا يوجد اتصال بالإنترنت');
                    }
                };
            }
            if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
            bar.style.display = 'flex';
            return;
        }
        bar.style.display = 'none';
        Object.values(verifyAbortControllers).forEach(controller => controller.abort());
        verifyAbortControllers = {};
        if (cancelBtn) cancelBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
        if (retryBtn) retryBtn.style.display = 'none';
        if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
    }

    function updateVerifyProgress(done, total) {
        const status = document.getElementById('verifyProgressStatus');
        const count = document.getElementById('verifyProgressCount');
        const fill = document.getElementById('verifyProgressFill');
        const retryBtn = document.getElementById('retryFailedBtn');
        const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('cancelVerificationBtn');
        const manualBtn = document.getElementById('manualVerifyBtn');
        if (!status || !count || !fill) return;
        if (!networkOnline) {
            status.textContent = '⚠️ غير متصل - انتظر عودة الإنترنت';
            count.textContent = '';
            fill.style.width = '0%';
            if (cancelBtn) cancelBtn.style.display = 'none';
            if (retryBtn) {
