                retryBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                retryBtn.textContent = '🔄 إعادة المحاولة';
                retryBtn.onclick = function() {
                    if (networkOnline) {
                        verifyCancelFlag = false;
                        cancelBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                        retryBtn.style.display = 'none';
                        if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
                        startCheckingAvailability();
                    } else {
                        toastWarning('لا يوجد اتصال بالإنترنت');
                    }
                };
            }
            if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
            showVerifyProgress();
            return;
        }
        if (done === 0 && total === 0) {
            status.textContent = '⏳ جاري التحقق...';
            count.textContent = '0/114';
            fill.style.width = '0%';
            if (retryBtn) retryBtn.style.display = 'none';
            if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
            return;
        }
        if (done >= total) {
            let failedCount = 0;
            const readerId = currentReader;
            if (surahAvailability[readerId]) {
                for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
                    if (surahAvailability[readerId][i] === 'failed') failedCount++;
                }
            }
            if (failedCount > 0) {
                status.textContent = `✅ تم التحقق ⚠️ ${failedCount} فشل`;
                if (retryBtn) {
                    retryBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                    retryBtn.textContent = `🔄 إعادة المحاولة (${failedCount})`;
                    retryBtn.onclick = function() {
                        retryFailedChecks();
                    };
                }
                if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
            } else {
                status.innerHTML = '✅ تم التحقق<br><span style="font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--gold);font-weight:600;">👆 اضغط <i data-icon="chart-simple"></i> للإحصائيات</span>';
                if (retryBtn) retryBtn.style.display = 'none';
                if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
                const statsBtn = document.getElementById('statsBtn');
                if (statsBtn) {
                    statsBtn.classList.add('pulse');
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        statsBtn.classList.remove('pulse');
                    }, 5000);
                }
                setTimeout(() => {
                    if (document.getElementById('verifyProgressBar')?.style.display !== 'none') {
                        hideVerifyProgress();
                    }
                }, 3000);
            }
            count.textContent = `${done}/${total}`;
            fill.style.width = '100%';
            return;
        }
        status.textContent = `⏳ جاري التحقق... (${Math.round((done/total)*100)}%)`;
        count.textContent = `${done}/${total}`;
        fill.style.width = `${(done/total)*100}%`;
        if (retryBtn) retryBtn.style.display = 'none';
        if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
        showVerifyProgress();
    }

    function retryFailedChecks() {
        const readerId = currentReader;
        if (!surahAvailability[readerId]) return;
        if (!networkOnline) {
            toastWarning('لا يوجد اتصال بالإنترنت');
            return;
        }
        let retryCount = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
            if (surahAvailability[readerId][i] === 'failed') {
                delete surahAvailability[readerId][i];
                checkSurahAvailability(readerId, i);
                retryCount++;
            }
        }
        if (retryCount > 0) {
            toastInfo(`جاري إعادة التحقق من ${retryCount} سورة...`);
        } else {
            toastInfo('لا توجد سور فاشلة لإعادة التحقق');
        }
    }

    document.getElementById('cancelVerificationBtn').addEventListener('click', function() {
        verifyCancelFlag = true;
        hideVerifyProgress();
        const retryBtn = document.getElementById('retryFailedBtn');
        const manualBtn = document.getElementById('manualVerifyBtn');
        const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('cancelVerificationBtn');
        if (retryBtn) {
            retryBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
            retryBtn.textContent = '🔄 إعادة التحقق';
            retryBtn.onclick = function() {
                if (!networkOnline) {
                    toastWarning('لا يوجد اتصال بالإنترنت');
                    return;
                }
                verifyCancelFlag = false;
                this.style.display = 'none';
                if (cancelBtn) cancelBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
                const readerId = currentReader;
                if (surahAvailability[readerId]) {
                    for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
                        if (surahAvailability[readerId][i] === 'failed' || 
                            surahAvailability[readerId][i] === 'unavailable' ||
                            surahAvailability[readerId][i] === 'pending') {
                            delete surahAvailability[readerId][i];
                        }
                    }
                }
                startCheckingAvailability();
                toastInfo('جاري التحقق من جديد...');
            };
        }
        if (manualBtn) manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
        toastInfo('تم إلغاء التحقق');
    });

    document.getElementById('retryFailedBtn').addEventListener('click', function() {
        retryFailedChecks();
    });

    document.getElementById('manualVerifyBtn').addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (!networkOnline) {
            toastWarning('لا يوجد اتصال بالإنترنت');
            return;
        }
        const readerId = currentReader;
        if (surahAvailability[readerId]) {
            for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
                if (surahAvailability[readerId][i] === 'failed' || surahAvailability[readerId][i] === 'unavailable') {
                    delete surahAvailability[readerId][i];
                }
            }
        }
        verifyCancelFlag = false;
        document.getElementById('cancelVerificationBtn').style.display = 'inline-flex';
        this.style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('retryFailedBtn').style.display = 'none';
        startCheckingAvailability();
        toastInfo('جاري التحقق من جديد...');
    });

    async function checkSurahAvailability(readerId, idx) {
        if (verifyCancelFlag) return;
        if (!networkOnline) {
            if (!surahAvailability[readerId]) surahAvailability[readerId] = {};
            surahAvailability[readerId][idx] = 'failed';
            updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
            updateGlobalVerifyProgress();
            return;
        }
        if (surahAvailability[readerId] && surahAvailability[readerId][idx] && surahAvailability[readerId][idx] !== 'pending') {
            updateGlobalVerifyProgress();
            return;
        }
        const url = getReaderUrl(readerId, idx);
        if (!url) {
            if (!surahAvailability[readerId]) surahAvailability[readerId] = {};
            surahAvailability[readerId][idx] = 'unavailable';
            updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
            updateGlobalVerifyProgress();
            return;
        }
        if (!surahAvailability[readerId]) surahAvailability[readerId] = {};
        surahAvailability[readerId][idx] = 'pending';
        updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
        updateGlobalVerifyProgress();
        const controller = new AbortController();
        const key = `${readerId}_${idx}`;
        verifyAbortControllers[key] = controller;
        try {
            const cache = await caches.open('quran-audio');
            const cachedResponse = await cache.match(url);
            if (cachedResponse) {
                surahAvailability[readerId][idx] = 'cached';
                if (!downloaded[readerId]) downloaded[readerId] = {};
                downloaded[readerId][idx] = { downloaded: true, size: 0, date: new Date().toISOString() };
                debouncedSaveState();
                updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
                updateGlobalVerifyProgress();
                delete verifyAbortControllers[key];
                return;
            }
            const response = await fetchWithTimeout(url, {
                method: 'HEAD',
                timeout: 5000,
                signal: controller.signal
            });
            if (response.ok) {
                surahAvailability[readerId][idx] = 'available';
                const size = parseInt(response.headers.get('content-length')) || 0;
                if (size > 0) fileSizeCache[url] = size;
            } else if (response.status === 404) {
                surahAvailability[readerId][idx] = 'unavailable';
            } else {
                surahAvailability[readerId][idx] = 'failed';
            }
        } catch (error) {
            if (error.name === 'AbortError') {
                delete surahAvailability[readerId][idx];
            } else {
                surahAvailability[readerId][idx] = 'failed';
            }
        }
        updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
        updateGlobalVerifyProgress();
        delete verifyAbortControllers[key];
    }

    function updateGlobalVerifyProgress() {
        const readerId = currentReader;
        if (!surahAvailability[readerId]) {
            hideVerifyProgress();
            return;
        }
        let total = 0;
        let done = 0;
        let available = 0;
        let unavailable = 0;
        let failed = 0;
        let cached = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
            const status = surahAvailability[readerId][i];
            if (status) {
                total++;
                done++;
                switch(status) {
                    case 'available': available++; break;
                    case 'cached': cached++; break;
                    case 'unavailable': unavailable++; break;
                    case 'failed': failed++; break;
                    case 'pending': done--; break;
                }
            }
        }
        verifyDone = done;
        verifyTotal = total;
        updateVerifyProgress(done, TOTAL_SURAHS);
        const retryBtn = document.getElementById('retryFailedBtn');
        const manualBtn = document.getElementById('manualVerifyBtn');
        if (retryBtn) {
            if (failed > 0 && done >= TOTAL_SURAHS) {
                retryBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                retryBtn.textContent = `🔄 إعادة المحاولة (${failed})`;
                retryBtn.onclick = function() {
                    retryFailedChecks();
                };
            } else if (done === 0 && total === 0 && !verifyCancelFlag) {
                retryBtn.style.display = 'none';
            } else {
                retryBtn.style.display = 'none';
            }
        }
        if (manualBtn) {
            if (verifyCancelFlag || (!networkOnline && done < TOTAL_SURAHS)) {
                manualBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
                manualBtn.textContent = '🔍 تحقق';
                manualBtn.onclick = function() {
                    if (!networkOnline) {
                        toastWarning('لا يوجد اتصال بالإنترنت');
                        return;
                    }
                    const readerId2 = currentReader;
                    if (surahAvailability[readerId2]) {
                        for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
                            if (surahAvailability[readerId2][i] === 'failed' || surahAvailability[readerId2][i] === 'unavailable') {
                                delete surahAvailability[readerId2][i];
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    verifyCancelFlag = false;
                    document.getElementById('cancelVerificationBtn').style.display = 'inline-flex';
                    manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
                    document.getElementById('retryFailedBtn').style.display = 'none';
                    startCheckingAvailability();
                    toastInfo('جاري التحقق من جديد...');
                };
            } else {
                manualBtn.style.display = 'none';
            }
        }
        if (done >= TOTAL_SURAHS && failed === 0 && !verifyCancelFlag) {
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (verifyDone >= verifyTotal && !verifyCancelFlag) hideVerifyProgress();
            }, 3000);
        }
    }

    function showDownloadConfirm(idx) {
        if (idx < 0 || idx > 113) { toastError('رقم سورة غير صحيح'); return; }
        confirmIdx = idx;
        var readerId = currentReader;
        var readerName = getReaderName(readerId);
        var surahName = surahNames[idx] || 'سورة ' + (idx + 1);
        document.getElementById('dlConfirmSurah').textContent = surahName;
        document.getElementById('dlConfirmReader').textContent = 'القارئ: ' + readerName;
        var sizeElement = document.getElementById('dlConfirmSize');
        sizeElement.textContent = '⏳ جاري حساب الحجم...';
        sizeElement.style.color = 'var(--text-secondary)';
        getFileSize(getReaderUrl(readerId, idx)).then(function(size) {
            var sizeText = size > 0 ? formatSize(size) : 'غير معروف';
            var sizeKB = size > 0 ? Math.round(size / 1024) : 0;
            var details = size > 0 ? ` (${sizeKB} ك.ب)` : '';
            sizeElement.innerHTML = `📦 الحجم: <strong>${sizeText}</strong>${details}`;
            sizeElement.style.color = 'var(--text)';
        }).catch(function() {
            sizeElement.textContent = '📦 الحجم: غير معروف (قد يكون صغيراً)';
            sizeElement.style.color = 'var(--text-secondary)';
        });
        document.getElementById('dlConfirmMulti').checked = false;
        document.getElementById('dlConfirmBtnText').textContent = 'تنزيل';
        openModalUniversal('downloadConfirmModal');
    }

    function hideDownloadConfirm() {
        closeModalUniversal('downloadConfirmModal');
    }

    function showSurahSelectionModal(initialIdx) {
        var modal = document.getElementById('surahSelectionModal'),
            grid = document.getElementById('selectionGrid');
        document.getElementById('selReaderName').textContent = getReaderName(currentReader);
        grid.innerHTML = '';
        var readerId = currentReader;
        for (var i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
            var div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'selection-item';
            var status = surahAvailability[readerId] && surahAvailability[readerId][i];
            var isAvailable = (status === 'available' || status === 'cached');
            var isDownloaded = downloaded[readerId] && downloaded[readerId][i] && downloaded[readerId][i]
                .downloaded;
            var checked = (i === initialIdx) && !isDownloaded && isAvailable;
            if (isDownloaded) div.classList.add('downloaded-item');
            div.innerHTML =
                '<input type="checkbox" data-idx="' + i + '" ' + (checked ? 'checked' : '') + (isDownloaded ?
                    ' disabled' : '') + (isAvailable ? '' : ' disabled') + '><span>' + (i + 1) + ' ' + surahNames[i] + '</span>' + (!isAvailable &&
                    !isDownloaded ? '<span class="unavailable-label">' + (status === 'failed' ? '⚠️' : '❌') + '</span>' : '') + (isDownloaded ?
                    '<span style="font-size:.6rem;color:var(--gold);">✅</span>' : '');
            var checkbox = div.querySelector('input');
            checkbox.addEventListener('change', updateSelectionStats);
            div.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                if (isDownloaded || !isAvailable) return;
                if (e.target.tagName !== 'INPUT') {
                    var cb = this.querySelector('input');
                    cb.checked = !cb.checked;
                    cb.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
                }
            });
            grid.appendChild(div);
        }
        document.getElementById('selTotal').textContent = '114';
        updateSelectionStats();
        openModalUniversal('surahSelectionModal');
    }

    function hideSurahSelectionModal() {
        closeModalUniversal('surahSelectionModal');
    }

    function updateSelectionStats() {
        var checked = document.querySelectorAll('#selectionGrid input[type="checkbox"]:checked');
        document.getElementById('selCount').textContent = checked.length;
        var readerId = currentReader;
        var totalSize = 0;
        checked.forEach(function(cb) {
            var idx = parseInt(cb.dataset.idx);
            var url = getReaderUrl(readerId, idx);
            if (url) {
                var size = getFileSizeFromCache(url);
                if (size > 0) totalSize += size;
            }
        });
        document.getElementById('selTotalSize').textContent = formatSize(totalSize) || '0';
    }

    var fileSizeCache = {};

    async function getFileSize(url) {
        if (fileSizeCache[url]) return fileSizeCache[url];
        try {
            var response = await fetchWithTimeout(url, { method: 'HEAD', timeout: 5000 });
            if (response.ok) {
                var size = parseInt(response.headers.get('content-length')) || 0;
                fileSizeCache[url] = size;
                return size;
            }
        } catch (e) {}
        return 0;
    }

    function getFileSizeFromCache(url) { return fileSizeCache[url] || 0; }

    async function downloadSelectedSurahs() {
        var checks = document.querySelectorAll('#selectionGrid input[type="checkbox"]:checked');
        var selected = [];
        checks.forEach(function(cb) {
            var idx = parseInt(cb.dataset.idx);
            var status = surahAvailability[currentReader] && surahAvailability[currentReader][idx];
            var isAvailable = (status === 'available' || status === 'cached');
            var isDownloaded = downloaded[currentReader] && downloaded[currentReader][idx] && downloaded[
                currentReader][idx].downloaded;
            if (isAvailable && !isDownloaded) selected.push(idx);
        });
        if (selected.length === 0) { toastWarning('لا توجد سور جديدة للتنزيل'); return; }
        hideSurahSelectionModal();
        await startMultipleDownloads(selected);
    }

    async function startMultipleDownloads(surahIndices) {
        if (isDownloadingMultiple) {
            toastWarning('جاري التنزيل بالفعل');
            return;
        }
        isDownloadingMultiple = true;
        downloadCancel = false;
        var readerId = currentReader;
        var readerName = getReaderName(readerId);
        var overlay = document.getElementById('downloadProgressOverlay');
        document.getElementById('dlProgressReader').textContent = readerName;
        overlay.classList.add('show');
        lockBodyScroll();
        document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.add('blurred');
        pushWindow('downloadProgressOverlay');
        var total = surahIndices.length;
        var done = 0,
            skipped = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < surahIndices.length; i++) {
            if (downloadCancel) break;
            var idx = surahIndices[i];
            var downloadId = `${readerId}_${idx}`;
            if (activeDownloads[downloadId]) {
                toastWarning(`التحميل جارٍ بالفعل لسورة ${surahNames[idx]}`);
                done++;
                continue;
            }
            var url = getReaderUrl(readerId, idx);
            if (!url) { done++;
                skipped++; continue; }
            var status = surahAvailability[readerId] && surahAvailability[readerId][idx];
            if (status === 'unavailable') { done++;
                skipped++; continue; }
            var isDl = downloaded[readerId] && downloaded[readerId][idx] && downloaded[readerId][idx]
                .downloaded;
            if (isDl) { done++; continue; }
            document.getElementById('dlProgressMain').textContent = Math.round((done / total) * 100) + '%';
            document.getElementById('dlProgressMainBar').style.width = (done / total) * 100 + '%';
            document.getElementById('dlProgressCount').textContent = done + '/' + total;
            document.getElementById('dlCurrentSurah').textContent = surahNames[idx];
            try {
                updateDownloadProgress(readerId, idx, 0);
                var result = await downloadWithProgress(url, function(prog) {
                    updateDownloadProgress(readerId, idx, prog);
                    document.getElementById('dlCurrentSurahBar').style.width = prog + '%';
                    document.getElementById('dlCurrentSurahPct').textContent = prog + '%';
                }, 0, downloadId);
                var blob = result.blob;
                var size = result.size || blob.size;
                var cache = await caches.open('quran-audio');
                await cache.put(url, new Response(blob));
                if (!downloaded[readerId]) downloaded[readerId] = {};
                downloaded[readerId][idx] = { downloaded: true, size: size, date: new Date()
                        .toISOString() };
                clearDownloadProgress(readerId, idx);
                debouncedSaveState();
                done++;
                await updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
                toastSuccess('تم تنزيل: ' + surahNames[idx]);
            } catch (e) {
                if (e.message === 'تم الإلغاء') {
                    toastInfo('تم إيقاف التنزيل');
                    break;
                }
                clearDownloadProgress(readerId, idx);
                delete activeDownloads[downloadId];
                done++;
                skipped++;
                toastError('فشل تنزيل: ' + surahNames[idx]);
            }
        }
        isDownloadingMultiple = false;
        closeModalUniversal('downloadProgressOverlay');
        unlockBodyScroll();
        document.getElementById('mainApp').classList.remove('blurred');
        renderAllSurahs();
        if (!downloadCancel) {
            if (skipped > 0) {
                toastSuccess('تم تنزيل ' + (done - skipped) + ' سورة' + (skipped > 0 ? '، تخطي ' + skipped +
                    ' سورة' : ''));
            } else if (done > 0) {
                toastSuccess('تم تنزيل ' + done + ' سورة بنجاح');
            }
        }
    }

    function downloadWithProgress(url, onProgress, startByte, downloadId) {
        return new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
            var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
            activeDownloads[downloadId] = { xhr: xhr, status: 'downloading' };
            activeXHR = xhr;
            if (startByte && startByte > 0) {
                xhr.open('GET', url, true);
                xhr.setRequestHeader('Range', 'bytes=' + startByte + '-');
            } else {
                xhr.open('GET', url, true);
            }
            xhr.responseType = 'blob';
            var totalSize = 0;
            xhr.onprogress = function(e) {
                if (e.lengthComputable) {
                    totalSize = e.total;
                    onProgress(Math.round((e.loaded / e.total) * 100));
                }
            };
            xhr.onload = function() {
                delete activeDownloads[downloadId];
                activeXHR = null;
                if (xhr.status === 200 || xhr.status === 206) {
                    var blob = xhr.response;
                    resolve({ blob: blob, size: blob.size });
                } else reject(new Error('فشل التنزيل'));
            };
            xhr.onerror = function() {
                delete activeDownloads[downloadId];
                activeXHR = null;
                reject(new Error('خطأ في الشبكة'));
            };
            xhr.onabort = function() {
                delete activeDownloads[downloadId];
                activeXHR = null;
                reject(new Error('تم الإلغاء'));
            };
            xhr.send();
        });
    }

    function updateDownloadProgress(readerId, idx, pct) {
        if (!downloadProgress[readerId]) downloadProgress[readerId] = {};
        downloadProgress[readerId][idx] = pct;
        updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
    }

    function clearDownloadProgress(readerId, idx) {
        if (downloadProgress[readerId]) delete downloadProgress[readerId][idx];
        updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
    }

    function abortCurrentDownload() {
        if (activeXHR) {
            activeXHR.abort();
            activeXHR = null;
        }
    }

    async function cancelPartialDownload(idx) {
        var readerId = currentReader;
        var url = getReaderUrl(readerId, idx);
        if (!url) return;
        abortCurrentDownload();
        var cache = await caches.open('quran-audio');
        await cache.delete(url);
        if (downloadProgress[readerId]) delete downloadProgress[readerId][idx];
        if (downloaded[readerId]) delete downloaded[readerId][idx];
        debouncedSaveState();
        await updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
        toastSuccess('تم إلغاء التنزيل الجزئي');
    }

    function handleDownloadClick(idx) {
        const readerId = currentReader;
        const downloadId = `${readerId}_${idx}`;
        const surahName = surahNames[idx];
        if (activeDownloads[downloadId]) {
            showAlertHtml(
                `⏳ التحميل جارٍ بالفعل لسورة <strong>"${surahName}"</strong>.<br>هل تريد إلغاء التحميل؟`,
                function() {
                    const downloadInfo = activeDownloads[downloadId];
                    if (downloadInfo && downloadInfo.xhr) {
                        downloadInfo.xhr.abort();
                        toastInfo('تم إلغاء تحميل "' + surahName + '"');
                        if (downloadProgress[readerId]) {
                            delete downloadProgress[readerId][idx];
                        }
                        updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
                    }
                }
            );
            return;
        }
        const url = getReaderUrl(readerId, idx);
        if (!url) {
            toastError('تعذر الحصول على رابط السورة');
            return;
        }
        caches.open('quran-audio').then(function(cache) {
            cache.match(url).then(function(cachedResponse) {
                if (cachedResponse) {
                    if (!downloaded[readerId]) downloaded[readerId] = {};
                    downloaded[readerId][idx] = { downloaded: true, size: 0, date: new Date().toISOString() };
                    debouncedSaveState();
                    updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
                    toastInfo('📀 "' + surahName + '" موجودة بالفعل في الذاكرة.');
                } else {
                    startNewDownload(readerId, idx);
                }
            });
        });
    }

    async function startNewDownload(readerId, idx) {
        const downloadId = `${readerId}_${idx}`;
        if (activeDownloads[downloadId]) {
            toastWarning('التحميل بدأ بالفعل');
            return;
        }
        const url = getReaderUrl(readerId, idx);
        if (!url) {
            toastError('تعذر الحصول على رابط السورة');
            return;
        }
        try {
            const cache = await caches.open('quran-audio');
            const cached = await cache.match(url);
            if (cached) {
                toastInfo('📀 السورة موجودة بالفعل في الذاكرة');
                if (!downloaded[readerId]) downloaded[readerId] = {};
                try {
                    const blob = await cached.blob();
                    downloaded[readerId][idx] = { downloaded: true, size: blob.size, date: new Date().toISOString() };
                } catch (e) {
                    downloaded[readerId][idx] = { downloaded: true, size: 0, date: new Date().toISOString() };
                }
                debouncedSaveState();
                await updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
                return;
            }
        } catch (e) {}
        const surahName = surahNames[idx];
        try {
            updateDownloadProgress(readerId, idx, 0);
            toastInfo('⬇️ جاري تنزيل: ' + surahName);
            var result = await downloadWithProgress(url, function(pct) {
                updateDownloadProgress(readerId, idx, pct);
            }, 0, downloadId);
            var blob = result.blob;
            var size = result.size || blob.size;
            var cache = await caches.open('quran-audio');
            await cache.put(url, new Response(blob));
            if (!downloaded[readerId]) downloaded[readerId] = {};
            downloaded[readerId][idx] = { downloaded: true, size: size, date: new Date().toISOString() };
            clearDownloadProgress(readerId, idx);
            debouncedSaveState();
            await updateSurahCardStatus(idx);
            var sizeText = formatSize(size);
            toastSuccess('✅ تم تنزيل "' + surahName + '" (' + sizeText + ')');
        } catch (e) {
            if (e.message === 'تم الإلغاء') {
                toastInfo('⏹️ تم إلغاء تنزيل: ' + surahName);
            } else {
                toastError('❌ فشل تنزيل: ' + surahName + ' (خطأ: ' + e.message + ')');
            }
            clearDownloadProgress(readerId, idx);
            delete activeDownloads[downloadId];
        }
    }

    function showDeleteConfirm(idx) {
        if (idx < 0 || idx > 113) { toastError('رقم سورة غير صحيح'); return; }
        confirmIdx = idx;
        var readerId = currentReader;
        var readerName = getReaderName(readerId);
        var surahName = surahNames[idx] || 'سورة ' + (idx + 1);
        var fileSize = downloaded[readerId]?.[idx]?.size || 0;
        var sizeElement = document.getElementById('dlDeleteSize');
        sizeElement.textContent = 'جاري حساب الحجم...';
        sizeElement.style.color = 'var(--text-secondary)';
        document.getElementById('dlDeleteSurah').textContent = surahName;
        document.getElementById('dlDeleteReader').textContent = 'القارئ: ' + readerName;
        if (fileSize > 0) {
            sizeElement.innerHTML = '📦 الحجم: <strong>' + formatSize(fileSize) + '</strong>';
            sizeElement.style.color = 'var(--text)';
        } else {
            var url = getReaderUrl(readerId, idx);
            if (url) {
                caches.open('quran-audio').then(function(cache) {
                    cache.match(url).then(function(response) {
                        if (response) {
                            response.blob().then(function(blob) {
                                var size = blob.size;
                                if (size > 0) {
                                    if (!downloaded[readerId]) downloaded[readerId] = {};
                                    if (downloaded[readerId][idx]) {
                                        downloaded[readerId][idx].size = size;
                                    } else {
                                        downloaded[readerId][idx] = { downloaded: true, size: size, date: new Date().toISOString() };
                                    }
                                    debouncedSaveState();
                                    sizeElement.innerHTML = '📦 الحجم: <strong>' + formatSize(size) + '</strong>';
                                    sizeElement.style.color = 'var(--text)';
                                } else {
                                    sizeElement.textContent = '📦 الحجم: غير معروف (ملف صغير جداً)';
                                    sizeElement.style.color = 'var(--text-secondary)';
                                }
                            }).catch(function() {
                                sizeElement.textContent = '📦 الحجم: غير معروف';
                                sizeElement.style.color = 'var(--text-secondary)';
                            });
                        } else {
                            sizeElement.textContent = '📦 الحجم: غير معروف (غير موجود في الذاكرة)';
                            sizeElement.style.color = 'var(--text-secondary)';
                        }
                    }).catch(function() {
                        sizeElement.textContent = '📦 الحجم: غير معروف';
                        sizeElement.style.color = 'var(--text-secondary)';
                    });
                });
            } else {
                sizeElement.textContent = '📦 الحجم: غير معروف';
                sizeElement.style.color = 'var(--text-secondary)';
            }
        }
        document.getElementById('dlDeleteMulti').checked = false;
        document.getElementById('dlDeleteBtnText').textContent = 'تأكيد الحذف';
        openModalUniversal('deleteConfirmModal');
    }

    function hideDeleteConfirm() {
        closeModalUniversal('deleteConfirmModal');
    }

    document.getElementById('dlDeleteOk').addEventListener('click', function() {
        var isMulti = document.getElementById('dlDeleteMulti').checked;
        if (isMulti) {
            hideDeleteConfirm();
            showDeleteSelectionModal();
        } else {
            var idx = confirmIdx;
            var readerId = currentReader;
            var surahName = surahNames[idx];
            var url = getReaderUrl(readerId, idx);
            if (url) {
                caches.open('quran-audio').then(function(cache) {
                    cache.delete(url);
                });
            }
            if (downloaded[readerId]) {
                delete downloaded[readerId][idx];
                if (Object.keys(downloaded[readerId]).length === 0) {
                    delete downloaded[readerId];
                }
            }
            if (surahAvailability[readerId]) {
                delete surahAvailability[readerId][idx];
            }
            debouncedSaveState();
            hideDeleteConfirm();
            renderAllSurahs(true);
            toastSuccess('تم حذف سورة "' + surahName + '"');
        }
    });

    document.getElementById('dlDeleteMulti').addEventListener('change', function() {
        document.getElementById('dlDeleteBtnText').textContent = this.checked ? 'اختيار السور' : 'تأكيد الحذف';
    });

    document.getElementById('dlDeleteCancel').addEventListener('click', hideDeleteConfirm);

    let deleteCaptchaCode = '';

    function showDeleteSelectionModal() {
        var modal = document.getElementById('deleteSelectionModal');
        var grid = document.getElementById('deleteSelectionGrid');
        document.getElementById('delSelReaderName').textContent = getReaderName(currentReader);
        grid.innerHTML = '';
        var readerId = currentReader;
        var hasDownloaded = false;
        for (var i = 0; i < TOTAL_SURAHS; i++) {
            var isDownloaded = downloaded[readerId] && downloaded[readerId][i] && downloaded[readerId][i].downloaded;
            if (isDownloaded) hasDownloaded = true;
            var div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'selection-item' + (isDownloaded ? '' : ' downloaded-item');
            var fileSize = isDownloaded ? (downloaded[readerId][i].size || 0) : 0;
            var sizeText = fileSize > 0 ? formatSize(fileSize) : '';
            div.innerHTML =
                '<input type="checkbox" data-idx="' + i + '" ' + (isDownloaded ? '' : 'disabled') + '>' +
                '<span>' + (i + 1) + ' ' + surahNames[i] + '</span>' +
                (isDownloaded ? 
                    '<span style="font-size:.6rem;color:var(--gold);margin-right:auto;">✅ ' + sizeText + '</span>' : 
                    '<span style="font-size:.6rem;color:var(--text-secondary);">❌ غير محملة</span>'
                );
            var checkbox = div.querySelector('input');
            checkbox.addEventListener('change', updateDeleteSelectionStats);
            div.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                if (e.target.tagName !== 'INPUT') {
                    var cb = this.querySelector('input');
                    if (!cb.disabled) {
                        cb.checked = !cb.checked;
                        cb.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
                    }
                }
            });
            grid.appendChild(div);
        }
        if (!hasDownloaded) {
            toastWarning('لا توجد سور محملة للحذف');
            return;
        }
        document.getElementById('delSelTotal').textContent = '114';
        updateDeleteSelectionStats();
        openModalUniversal('deleteSelectionModal');
    }

    function updateDeleteSelectionStats() {
        var checked = document.querySelectorAll('#deleteSelectionGrid input[type="checkbox"]:checked');
        document.getElementById('delSelCount').textContent = checked.length;
        var readerId = currentReader;
        var totalSize = 0;
        checked.forEach(function(cb) {
            var idx = parseInt(cb.dataset.idx);
            if (downloaded[readerId] && downloaded[readerId][idx]) {
                totalSize += downloaded[readerId][idx].size || 0;
            }
        });
        document.getElementById('delSelTotalSize').textContent = totalSize > 0 ? formatSize(totalSize) : '0';
    }

    function hideDeleteSelectionModal() {
        closeModalUniversal('deleteSelectionModal');
    }

    function showDeleteVerificationModal() {
        var checked = document.querySelectorAll('#deleteSelectionGrid input[type="checkbox"]:checked');
        if (checked.length === 0) {
            toastWarning('لم تختر أي سورة للحذف');
            return;
        }
        closeModalUniversal('deleteSelectionModal');
        deleteCaptchaCode = generateRandomCode();
        document.getElementById('deleteCaptchaCode').textContent = deleteCaptchaCode;
        document.getElementById('deleteCaptchaInput').value = '';
        document.getElementById('deleteVerifyError').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('deleteVerifyCount').textContent = checked.length;
        openModalUniversal('deleteVerifyModal');
        setTimeout(function() { document.getElementById('deleteCaptchaInput').focus(); }, 300);
    }

    function executeDelete() {
        var input = document.getElementById('deleteCaptchaInput').value.trim();
        if (input !== deleteCaptchaCode) {
            document.getElementById('deleteVerifyError').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('deleteCaptchaInput').value = '';
            deleteCaptchaCode = generateRandomCode();
            document.getElementById('deleteCaptchaCode').textContent = deleteCaptchaCode;
            setTimeout(function() { document.getElementById('deleteVerifyError').style.display = 'none'; }, 3000);
            return;
        }
        closeModalUniversal('deleteVerifyModal');
        var checked = document.querySelectorAll('#deleteSelectionGrid input[type="checkbox"]:checked');
        var readerId = currentReader;
        var deleted = 0;
        var totalSize = 0;
        var deletedNames = [];
        checked.forEach(function(cb) {
            var idx = parseInt(cb.dataset.idx);
            var surahName = surahNames[idx];
            var url = getReaderUrl(readerId, idx);
            if (url) {
                caches.open('quran-audio').then(function(cache) {
                    cache.delete(url);
                });
            }
            if (downloaded[readerId] && downloaded[readerId][idx]) {
                totalSize += downloaded[readerId][idx].size || 0;
                delete downloaded[readerId][idx];
                deleted++;
                deletedNames.push(surahName);
            }
            if (surahAvailability[readerId]) {
                delete surahAvailability[readerId][idx];
            }
        });
        if (Object.keys(downloaded[readerId]).length === 0) {
            delete downloaded[readerId];
        }
        debouncedSaveState();
        renderAllSurahs(true);
        var sizeText = totalSize > 0 ? formatSize(totalSize) : '';
        toastSuccess('✅ تم حذف ' + deleted + ' سورة' + (sizeText ? ' (' + sizeText + ')' : ''));
        if (deleted <= 5) {
            toastInfo('السور المحذوفة: ' + deletedNames.join('، '));
        }
    }

    document.getElementById('delSelSelectAll').addEventListener('click', function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('#deleteSelectionGrid input[type="checkbox"]:not(:disabled)').forEach(function(cb) {
            cb.checked = true;
        });
        updateDeleteSelectionStats();
    });

    document.getElementById('delSelDeselectAll').addEventListener('click', function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('#deleteSelectionGrid input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(function(cb) {
            cb.checked = false;
        });
        updateDeleteSelectionStats();
    });

    document.getElementById('delSelCancel').addEventListener('click', function() {
        hideDeleteSelectionModal();
        showDeleteConfirm(confirmIdx);
    });

    document.getElementById('delSelDelete').addEventListener('click', showDeleteVerificationModal);

    document.getElementById('deleteVerifyCancel').addEventListener('click', function() {
        closeModalUniversal('deleteVerifyModal');
        showDeleteSelectionModal();
    });

    document.getElementById('deleteVerifyConfirm').addEventListener('click', executeDelete);

    document.getElementById('deleteCaptchaInput').addEventListener('input', function() {
        this.value = this.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
    });

    document.getElementById('deleteCaptchaInput').addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            executeDelete();
        }
    });

    var sleepTimerInterval = null,
        sleepTimerRemaining = 0;

    function stopSleepTimer() {
        if (sleepTimerInterval) {
            clearInterval(sleepTimerInterval);
            sleepTimerInterval = null;
        }
        sleepTimerRemaining = 0;
        document.getElementById('sleepTimerIndicator').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('sleepTimerDisplay').textContent = '00:00';
        document.getElementById('sleepTimerBtn').innerHTML = '<i data-icon="hourglass-half"></i>';
        applyIconsToAll();
    }

    function updateTimerDisplay() {
        var mins = Math.floor(sleepTimerRemaining / 60);
        var secs = sleepTimerRemaining % 60;
        var timeStr = String(mins).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(secs).padStart(2, '0');
        document.getElementById('sleepTimerDisplay').textContent = timeStr;
    }

    function startSleepTimer(minutes, seconds) {
        stopSleepTimer();
        var totalSeconds = (minutes * 60) + seconds;
        if (totalSeconds < 1) { toastWarning('الحد الأدنى ثانية واحدة'); return; }
        sleepTimerRemaining = totalSeconds;
        document.getElementById('sleepTimerIndicator').style.display = 'flex';
        updateTimerDisplay();
        toastInfo('تم ضبط مؤقت النوم على ' + String(minutes).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(seconds).padStart(2,
            '0'));
        sleepTimerInterval = setInterval(function() {
            sleepTimerRemaining--;
            updateTimerDisplay();
            if (sleepTimerRemaining <= 0) {
                stopSleepTimer();
                toastInfo('انتهى وقت المؤقت، تم إيقاف التشغيل');
                if (audio.src && !audio.paused) { audio.pause();
                    isPlaying = false;
                    updatePlayerUI(); }
                if (radioAudio.src && !radioAudio.paused) { radioAudio.pause();
                    isRadio = false;
                    isRadioPlaying = false;
                    updateRadioBtnUI(); }
            }
        }, 1000);
    }

    var APK_URL = 'https://mamhaz.github.io/QuranKarim/QuranKarimv2.apk';
    var manifest = {
        name: "القرآن الكريم المتقدم",
        short_name: "القرآن الكريم",
        description: "استمع واقرأ القرآن مع أكثر من 100 قارئ وتفسير",
        start_url: ".",
        display: "standalone",
        background_color: "#f5f0eb",
        theme_color: "#c9a84c",
        dir: "rtl",
        lang: "ar",
        version: "3.0.0",
        icons: [
            { src: "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/MAmhaz/QuranKarim/refs/heads/main/QuranKarim.png",
                sizes: "512x512", type: "image/png", purpose: "any maskable" },
            { src: "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/MAmhaz/QuranKarim/refs/heads/main/QuranKarim.png",
                sizes: "192x192", type: "image/png", purpose: "any maskable" }
        ]
    };
    var blobManifest = new Blob([JSON.stringify(manifest)], { type: 'application/json' });
    var manifestURL = URL.createObjectURL(blobManifest);
    var linkManifest = document.createElement('link');
    linkManifest.rel = 'manifest';
    linkManifest.href = manifestURL;
    document.head.appendChild(linkManifest);

    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
        var swCode =
            '\nconst CACHE_NAME = \'quran-v3\';\nconst urlsToCache = [ \'.\', \'index.html\' ];\nself.addEventListener(\'install\', e => {\n e.waitUntil(\n caches.open(CACHE_NAME)\n .then(cache => cache.addAll(urlsToCache))\n .then(() => self.skipWaiting())\n );\n});\nself.addEventListener(\'activate\', e => {\n e.waitUntil(\n caches.keys().then(keys => Promise.all(\n keys.filter(k => k !== CACHE_NAME).map(k => caches.delete(k))\n ))\n );\n});\nself.addEventListener(\'fetch\', e => {\n e.respondWith(\n caches.match(e.request)\n .then(response => response || fetch(e.request))\n );\n});\n';
        var swBlob = new Blob([swCode], { type: 'application/javascript' });
        var swURL = URL.createObjectURL(swBlob);
        navigator.serviceWorker.register(swURL, { scope: '.' }).then(function() {
            URL.revokeObjectURL(swURL);
        }).catch(function() {
            navigator.serviceWorker.register('service-worker.js', { scope: '.' }).catch(function() {});
        });
    }

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        deferredPrompt = e;
        var pwaBtn = document.getElementById('installPWA');
        if (pwaBtn) {
            pwaBtn.classList.remove('option-disabled');
            pwaBtn.style.opacity = '1';
            pwaBtn.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
        }
    });

    var installAppBtn = document.getElementById('installAppBtn'),
        installModal = document.getElementById('installModal'),
        installPWA = document.getElementById('installPWA'),
        installAPK = document.getElementById('installAPK'),
        installModalClose = document.getElementById('installModalClose');

    if (installAppBtn) installAppBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (isWebViewEnv) {
            window.open(APK_URL, '_blank');
            toastInfo('جاري تحميل ملف التثبيت...');
            return;
        }
        if (installModal) {
            openModalUniversal('installModal');
            if (!deferredPrompt) {
                var pwaBtn = document.getElementById('installPWA');
                if (pwaBtn) {
                    pwaBtn.classList.add('option-disabled');
                    pwaBtn.style.opacity = '0.5';
                    pwaBtn.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
                }
            } else {
                var pwaBtn = document.getElementById('installPWA');
                if (pwaBtn) {
                    pwaBtn.classList.remove('option-disabled');
                    pwaBtn.style.opacity = '1';
                    pwaBtn.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                }
            }
        }
    });

    if (installPWA) installPWA.addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (deferredPrompt) {
            deferredPrompt.prompt();
            deferredPrompt.userChoice.then(function(choice) {
                if (choice.outcome === 'accepted') toastSuccess('تم تثبيت التطبيق');
                else toastInfo('تم إلغاء التثبيت');
                deferredPrompt = null;
                if (installModal) {
                    closeModalUniversal('installModal');
                }
            });
        }
    });

    if (installAPK) installAPK.addEventListener('click', function() {
        window.open(APK_URL, '_blank');
        toastInfo('جاري تحميل ملف التثبيت...');
        if (installModal) {
            closeModalUniversal('installModal');
        }
    });

    if (installModalClose) installModalClose.addEventListener('click', function() {
        closeModalUniversal('installModal');
    });

    var textScale = parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('textScale') || '1'),
        displayScale = parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('displayScale') || '1');

    function applyScales() {
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--text-scale', textScale);
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--display-scale', displayScale);
        document.getElementById('textSizeValue').textContent = Math.round(textScale * 100) + '%';
        document.getElementById('displaySizeValue').textContent = Math.round(displayScale * 100) + '%';
        localStorage.setItem('textScale', String(textScale));
        localStorage.setItem('displayScale', String(displayScale));
        document.querySelectorAll('#readerContent .ayah-text').forEach(function(el) { el.style.fontSize =
                fontsize + 'rem'; });
    }

    document.getElementById('textSizeInc').addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (textScale < 1.4) {
            textScale += 0.1;
            applyScales();
            toastInfo('حجم النص: ' + Math.round(textScale * 100) + '%');
        }
    });

    document.getElementById('textSizeDec').addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (textScale > 0.6) {
            textScale -= 0.1;
            applyScales();
            toastInfo('حجم النص: ' + Math.round(textScale * 100) + '%');
        }
    });

    document.getElementById('displaySizeInc').addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (displayScale < 1.4) {
            displayScale += 0.1;
            applyScales();
            toastInfo('حجم العرض: ' + Math.round(displayScale * 100) + '%');
        }
    });

    document.getElementById('displaySizeDec').addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (displayScale > 0.6) {
            displayScale -= 0.1;
            applyScales();
            toastInfo('حجم العرض: ' + Math.round(displayScale * 100) + '%');
        }
    });

    applyScales();

    var initialReader = getReader(currentReader);
    if (initialReader) {
        var nameEl = document.getElementById('selectedReaderName');
        if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = initialReader.name;
    }

    audio.volume = volume;
    radioAudio.volume = volume;
    audio.playbackRate = speed;
    if (speedBtn) speedBtn.textContent = speed + 'x';

    var repLabels = { off: 'إيقاف', one: 'تكرار سورة', all: 'تكرار الكل' };
    if (repeatBtn) repeatBtn.innerHTML = '<i data-icon="redo"></i> ' + repLabels[repeatMode];

    renderReaderList();
    surahAvailability[currentReader] = {};
    calculateAllSurahSizes().then(function() {
        renderAllSurahs();
    });
    loadQuranTexts();
    restoreLast();

    var sleepTimerBtn = document.getElementById('sleepTimerBtn'),
        sleepTimerModal = document.getElementById('sleepTimerModal'),
        sleepTimerClose = document.getElementById('sleepTimerClose');

    if (sleepTimerBtn && sleepTimerModal) {
        sleepTimerBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            document.getElementById('sleepMinutes').textContent = '00';
            document.getElementById('sleepSeconds').textContent = '00';
            openModalUniversal('sleepTimerModal');
        });

        document.querySelectorAll('#sleepTimerModal .arrow-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
            btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                var target = this.dataset.target;
                var dir = this.dataset.dir;
                var el = document.getElementById(target === 'minutes' ? 'sleepMinutes' :
                    'sleepSeconds');
                var val = parseInt(el.textContent);
                if (dir === 'up') val = (val + 1) % 60;
                else val = (val - 1 + 60) % 60;
                el.textContent = String(val).padStart(2, '0');
                var minutes = parseInt(document.getElementById('sleepMinutes').textContent);
                var seconds = parseInt(document.getElementById('sleepSeconds').textContent);
                if (minutes === 0 && seconds === 0) {
                    toastWarning('الحد الأدنى ثانية واحدة');
                    document.getElementById('sleepSeconds').textContent = '01';
                }
            });
        });

        document.getElementById('sleepTimerStart').addEventListener('click', function() {
            var minutes = parseInt(document.getElementById('sleepMinutes').textContent);
            var seconds = parseInt(document.getElementById('sleepSeconds').textContent);
            if (minutes === 0 && seconds === 0) { toastWarning('الحد الأدنى ثانية واحدة'); return; }
            startSleepTimer(minutes, seconds);
            closeModalUniversal('sleepTimerModal');
        });

        document.getElementById('sleepTimerCancel').addEventListener('click', function() {
            stopSleepTimer();
            closeModalUniversal('sleepTimerModal');
            toastInfo('تم إلغاء مؤقت النوم');
        });

        if (sleepTimerClose) sleepTimerClose.addEventListener('click', function() {
            closeModalUniversal('sleepTimerModal');
        });
    }

    window.addEventListener('resize', updateFooterHeight);

    window.addEventListener('load', function() {
        updateFooterHeight();
        if (!document.querySelector('.surah-card')) {
            renderAllSurahs();
        }
        loadFontAwesome();
    });

    if (lastSurah >= 0 && lastTime > 0) {
        var url = getReaderUrl(currentReader, lastSurah);
        if (url) {
            getCachedUrl(url).then(function(cachedUrl) {
                audio.src = cachedUrl || url;
                currentSurah = lastSurah;
                audio.currentTime = lastTime;
                var infoEl = $('playerInfo');
                if (infoEl) infoEl.textContent = '';
                var playerEl = $('bottomPlayer');
                if (playerEl) {
                    playerEl.classList.add('visible');
                    updateFooterHeight();
                }
                updatePlayerTitle();
                updatePlayerFavButton();
            });
        }
    }

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function() {
        if (document.hidden) {
            debouncedSaveState();
            if (isReaderOpen) addSession('reader', currentReader, currentSurah, 0);
            else if (currentSurah >= 0 && isPlaying) addSession('surah', currentReader, currentSurah,
                audio.currentTime);
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', function() {
        debouncedSaveState();
        if (isReaderOpen) addSession('reader', currentReader, currentSurah, 0);
        else if (currentSurah >= 0 && isPlaying) addSession('surah', currentReader, currentSurah, audio
            .currentTime);
    });

    if ('ResizeObserver' in window) {
        var _playerEl = document.getElementById('bottomPlayer');
        var _footerEl = document.getElementById('fixedFooter');
        var _roCallback = function() { updateFooterHeight(); };
        if (_playerEl) new ResizeObserver(_roCallback).observe(_playerEl);
        if (_footerEl) new ResizeObserver(_roCallback).observe(_footerEl);
    }

    var tafsirCloseBtn = document.getElementById('tafsirClose');
    if (tafsirCloseBtn) {
        tafsirCloseBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            closeModalUniversal('tafsirModal');
        });
    }

    console.log('✅ تم تحميل التطبيق بنجاح مع النظام الهجين');
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
